## Möbius hyperbola product estimate

[HyperbolaProduct.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/HyperbolaProduct.lean) proves the finite product-grouping and complementary-divisor identities for

```math
a_N(k)=\sum_{\substack{d\mid k\\k^2\le Nd}}\mu(d).
```

The [written proof](successor-and-division/hyperbola-product.md) applies classical Fourier and derivative estimates to obtain, for every $\delta>0$ and integer $\sqrt N\le A\le N/2$,

```math
\left|\sum_{\sqrt N<k\le A}a_N(k)
\left(\{N/k\}-\tfrac12\right)\right|
\ll_\delta N^\delta A^{4/3}N^{-1/3}.
```

Thus products through $N^{5/8}$ contribute $O_\delta(N^{1/2+\delta})$. The analytic estimate is not formalized in Lean. Larger products and the other terms in the prime-error formula remain unresolved. Exact publication priority is unestablished; this entry records a useful application of classical methods.

[Sources and proof](successor-and-division/hyperbola-product.md) · [Verification](../formalization/verification/hyperbola-product.json).

## A regular source with a large Möbius inverse

[RegularSource.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/RegularSource.lean) proves that for each real $r<2/3$ there is a single sequence $f:\mathbb N\to\mathbb R$ satisfying, for every $q\ge0$,

```math
|f(q)|\le\sqrt q,\qquad |f(q+1)-f(q)|\le1,
```

whose Möbius floor inverse has no eventual bound of order $N^r$:

```math
\nexists C>0,\,N_0\ge1\quad
\forall N\ge N_0:\quad
\left|\sum_{d=1}^{N}\mu(d)f\!\left(\left\lfloor N/d\right\rfloor\right)\right|
\le C N^r.
```

The [complete written proof](prime-distribution/regular-source-mobius-obstruction.md) retains both the finite construction and the fixed-source argument. The sequence may depend on $r$. At $N=t^3$, separated tent functions first give a sequence with inverse at least $t^2/64$ for $t\ge256$. The Banach–Steinhaus theorem then yields a fixed sequence for each exponent.

This excludes deriving the desired inverse bound from size and bounded increments alone. It does not give a lower bound for the particular arithmetic source $H$, or settle RH.

The proof uses elementary squarefree counting and the classical uniform boundedness principle. Its formal dependencies include mathlib's [Banach–Steinhaus theorem](https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/f897ebcf72cd16f89ab4577d0c826cd14afaafc7/Mathlib/Analysis/Normed/Operator/BanachSteinhaus.lean), by Jireh Loreaux, and [bounded-function normed spaces](https://github.com/leanprover-community/mathlib4/blob/f897ebcf72cd16f89ab4577d0c826cd14afaafc7/Mathlib/Topology/ContinuousMap/Bounded/Normed.lean), by Sébastien Gouëzel, Mario Carneiro, Yury Kudryashov, and Heather Macbeth. These dependencies retain their Apache 2.0 license. Priority of this exact counterexample and its formalization is unestablished; no originality claim is made.

[Verification](../formalization/verification/regular-source.json).

## Finite primitive-energy bounds

[CoarsePrimitive.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CoarsePrimitive.lean) bounds weighted integrals of the actual prime error $`e(x)=\psi(\lfloor x\rfloor_+)-x`$. Put $`P_X(t)=\int_X^t e(x)\,dx`$, $`R_X(t)=\int_t^{2X}e(x)\,dx`$ and $`S_X=\int_X^{2X}(P_X(t)^2+R_X(t)^2)\,dt`$.

For $`X>0`$, a continuous complex test function $`w`$ differentiable inside the block, with interval-integrable derivative satisfying $`\lVert w'\rVert\le K`$ for $`K\ge0`$, obeys

```math
\left\lVert\int_X^{2X}e(x)w(x)\,dx\right\rVert
\le\sqrt{2S_X/X}\,\lVert w(2X)\rVert+K\sqrt{XS_X}.
```

The module also proves the exact finite Mellin identity, retaining the terminal mass and integer jumps. It supplies no growth bound for $`S_X`$ or infinite-series continuation theorem. The mathematics uses classical Cauchy–Schwarz and one-sided integration by parts.

[Assumptions, use and attribution](prime-distribution/coarse-primitive.md) · [Written RH criterion](prime-distribution/coarse-energy-rh-criterion.md) · [Verification](../formalization/verification/coarse-primitive.json).

## Exact hyperbola endpoint correction

[HyperbolaEndpoint.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/HyperbolaEndpoint.lean) evaluates the complete signed endpoint count, for every integer $`N\ge1`$:

```math
E(N)=\sum_{d\mid N}\mu(d)\#\{m\mid N/d:m\le\sqrt{N/d}\},
\qquad 2E(N)=1+(-1)^{\Omega(N)}.
```

Here $`\Omega`$ counts prime factors with multiplicity. The original inclusive double-sum equality is also formalized. The full ordinary-to-midpoint sawtooth correction is consequently zero or $`-1/2`$; that real-valued conversion is a written consequence and does not apply to arbitrary restricted outer blocks. The proof uses classical divisor pairing and Möbius inversion.

[Statement, use and attribution](analytic-methods/hyperbola-endpoint.md) · [Verification](../formalization/verification/hyperbola-endpoint.json).

## A conditional route to full RH

[CoarsePrimitiveCriterion.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CoarsePrimitiveCriterion.lean) proves that the actual primitive energy bound $`S_{2^k}\le C_\epsilon2^{k(4+\epsilon)}`$, for every $`\epsilon>0`$ and every $`k\ge0`$, implies mathlib's full Riemann hypothesis. The energy bound remains unproved. The formal proof includes local uniform convergence, the zeta identity and exclusion of all nontrivial zeros off the critical line under that hypothesis.

[Statement, proof and attribution](zeta-and-zeros/coarse-conditional-rh.md) · [Verification](../formalization/verification/coarse-conditional-result.json).

[MertensPrimeTransfer.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/MertensPrimeTransfer.lean) converts a bound on the actual Mertens function at every quotient $`\lfloor N/k\rfloor`$, $`1\le k\le K`$, into an explicit bound for $`|\psi(N)-N+2\gamma|`$. The finite hyperbola and partial-summation proof retains its boundary term and all prime powers. The Mertens premise remains to be proved by a consumer.

## Angular height and two boundary ranges

The [written theorem](analytic-methods/angular-height-boundary.md) bounds the angular height of a cyclotomic prefix by a signed sum of old-root derivative-deficit updates. Exact Farey insertion gives a polylogarithmic positive-birth allowance. Conductors at most $`\sqrt X`$ and ages at most $`\sqrt N`$ have total absolute cost $`O(\sqrt X\log^3X)`$.

The remaining interior interaction is unbounded. The proof uses classical chord concavity, Farey geometry and divisor sums; it has no Lean formalization or originality claim. [Exact finite checks](../formalization/verification/angular-boundary-result.json) · [Provenance](../formalization/verification/angular-boundary-provenance.json).

## Local two-history forcing

[TwoHistoryForcing.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/TwoHistoryForcing.lean) proves the exact mixed response of $h(n)=\log n-\tau(n)+2\gamma$ on coprime factors, its nonpositive sign, and finite Bonferroni bounds for higher interaction coefficients. These local identities do not bound the full signed prime-error energy.

[Statements, proof and sources](prime-history/two-history-forcing.md).

## GCD threshold kernels

The [written proof](analytic-methods/gcd-threshold-kernels.md) gives the signed divisor-square decomposition of $\mathbf1_{\omega(\gcd(k,l))\ge2}$ and shows that fixed weights on its zero-, one-, and at-least-two-prime levels yield a universally positive semidefinite kernel only when all three weights are equal and nonnegative. It identifies why a pointwise nonnegative arithmetic kernel need not define a positive quadratic form. No Lean formalization is claimed.

## Cyclotomic eta finite parts

The [written proof](geometry/cyclotomic-eta-finite-parts.md) evaluates the rational-cusp normalization of an eta product with Mertens exponents and its exact tail correction. The correction retains the original cyclotomic derivative, so the modular transformation alone supplies no additional phase-dependent cancellation. No Lean formalization is claimed.

## Theta cutoff completion obstruction

The [written proof](theta-and-heat/theta-cutoff-completion.md) shows that every finite theta cutoff, and each specified positive even square-root completion, has infinitely many nonreal Fourier-transform zeros at every real heat time. It identifies the endpoint defect and its complex branch contribution despite rapid weighted kernel approximation. The result concerns these approximation families, not zeros of the full Riemann xi function. It uses classical contour asymptotics and Hadamard factorization; no originality or Lean formalization is claimed.

## Shifted Fourier correlations

The [written proof](prime-distribution/shifted-fourier-density.md) distinguishes an analytic Wronskian from derivatives of the squared Fourier modulus. A positive analytic Bessel-convolution kernel disproves the general density assertion with the outside-hyperbolic-cosine kernel in Dimitrov–Xu arXiv:1606.05011v1, Theorem 3.2. The corrected kernel yields a density criterion allowing multiple real zeros. No conclusion about the truth of RH, originality, or Lean formalization is claimed.

## Theta radial-weight positivity

The [written proof](theta-and-heat/theta-radial-positivity.md) shows that every complete radial weight in the actual folded theta expansion is strictly positive, for all real Fourier arguments and every positive vertical shift. An exact Abel/Bessel square identity retains the lower endpoint and all differential terms. The remaining divisor coefficients are signed; positivity of their full sum is unproved. No originality or Lean formalization claim is made.

## Cosh-base universal-factor approximation obstruction

The [written proof](theta-and-heat/cosh-universal-factor-closure.md) excludes even local positive-axis $L^1$ approximation of the actual theta kernel by $e^{-a_j\cosh(b_ju)}$ times positive even Pólya universal factors, including all parameter escape. Logarithmic curvature forces an entire limit, contradicting a theta boundary singularity. Arbitrary real-zero Fourier approximants remain outside the conclusion. No originality or Lean formalization claim is made.

## An eventual-sign criterion from distinct-prime convolution

The [written proof](prime-distribution/distinct-prime-sign-criterion.md) shows that RH is equivalent to eventual negativity of a centered triangular multiplicative convolution after subtracting the contributions from powers of the same prime. A weighted Mellin boundary estimate retains arbitrary zero multiplicities and proves that the negative prime-square bias dominates under RH. The unconditional sign remains unproved, and no Lean formalization or originality claim is made.

## A complete Bernstein obstruction for theta Mellin interpolation

The [written proof](theta-and-heat/theta-mellin-bernstein-obstruction.md) and [Arb certificate](../certificates/theta_mellin_laguerre.py) show that an auxiliary normalized Mellin transform of the complete theta kernel violates the Laguerre inequality and has a nonreal zero. Its entire continuation then excludes every complete Bernstein function matching the actual xi coefficient ratios, including the KPS one-separation subclass. General Bernstein, $B_J$, $D_P$ and RH remain unresolved; this is an Arb-assisted proof, not a Lean formalization.

## Square-root aggregation of the sign criterion

The [written proof](zeta-and-zeros/sqrt-multiple-sign-criterion.md) proves that RH is equivalent to eventual negativity of the square-root-weighted sum over multiples of the distinct-prime convolution. Under RH its main term is $-x^{3/2}\log^2x/6$, with error $O(x^{3/2}\log x)$. The proof preserves arbitrary zero multiplicities and needs no finite simplicity verification. The unconditional sign remains open.

## Undamped prime products in Nyman–Beurling approximation

The [written proof](prime-distribution/prime-product-nyman-test.md) shows that finite undamped prime-division products fail in the full Nyman–Beurling Hilbert norm even after optimal scalar normalization: the best squared error tends to one. Locally convergent scalar choices instead have divergent full error. This tests that family of approximants, not the criterion itself.

## Local Möbius signs and dense history energy

The [written proof](prime-history/partial-mobius-dense-history.md) uses the classical truncated Möbius weights with the actual divisor forcing. They obey every division-sign rule up to a growing polylogarithmic prime cutoff, yet their complete prefix/suffix energy has order $X^5$ up to logarithmic factors. Removing all those small-prime edges leaves the same leading cross energy. These are auxiliary weights, not the actual Möbius function.

## Cyclotomic activity, quadrature and boundary reductions

The [written proof](geometry/cyclotomic-activity-quadrature.md) separates the clipped old-root update into its exact angular correlation, mesh flux and curvature subtraction. A uniform cyclic-cell estimate bounds the cumulative interior geometric remainder by $12\sqrt X\log((3\pi/4)X^2)$; reduced conductor and reduced age boundary sums are controlled absolutely. An [explicit activity-selection example](geometry/cyclotomic-activity-necessity.md) shows why bounded symmetric activities alone cannot control the remaining correlation. Actual derivative history remains essential.

## Incomplete-box carry covariance

The [written proof](successor-and-division/incomplete-carry-covariance.md) reduces covariance of two modular carries to flat and triangular one-dimensional sawtooth correlations, with endpoint error at most $(d+e)/(8H)$. Its exact Fourier formula retains incomplete periods and all residue-zero corrections.

The [written proof](prime-distribution/divisor-observable-entropy.md) gives exact CRT entropy and cyclic shift-energy formulas for $\tau(\gcd(n,r))$. It proves an exponent-uniform anchored entropy comparison with $\log(\sigma(n)/n)$ and a primorial obstruction to a constant observable log-Sobolev bound.

## The actual theta arcsine mixing law

The [written proof](theta-and-heat/theta-arcsine-law.md) recovers the unique positive arcsine scale law of the actual theta density, its exact tail and its only possible Lévy exponent. Membership of that exponent in $B_J$ remains unproved. The mixing law is not additively infinitely divisible; this does not exclude an exponential-functional representation.

The [explicit Bessel example](theta-and-heat/noncomplete-bernstein-real-zero-example.md) belongs to $B_J$ but not the complete Bernstein class, while its associated entire characteristic function has only real zeros. The construction concerns an auxiliary function and supplies no class-membership theorem for the actual xi function.

A [finite neighborhood argument](successor-and-division/prime-division-unmatched.md), combined with Selberg’s squarefree-factor count, forces at least $(3/(\pi^2\sqrt{2\pi})+o(1))N/\sqrt{\log\log N}$ unmatched vertices in the one-prime division graph. Neither statement lower-bounds the actual signed error.

The [written proof](prime-history/integrated-divisor-history.md) keeps exact fractional endpoints, evaluates the common drift through integrated Voronoï theory, and proves that all labels up to $\sqrt X$ can be left unmatched within an $O(X^4)$ energy budget. It also gives sharp per-edge tests showing where uniform stronger savings fail.

The [written proof](prime-distribution/integrated-prime-channel-triangle.md) gives an elementary $cX^{5/2}$ lower bound for the sum of the integrated largest-prime-channel norms, while retaining the exact density-inclusive finite Gram formula for the signed aggregate. It excludes that triangle majorant alone.

## Restrictions on theta multiplier constructions

The [Bessel heat and Hankel tests](theta-and-heat/theta-bessel-factorization-obstructions.md) exclude specified positive-heat mixtures and sufficient radial truncation criteria for the actual theta law. The [Euler–Gaussian factorization theorem](theta-and-heat/theta-operator-factorization-obstructions.md) excludes a specified sample-interpolation class and its coefficientwise closure, using Carlson uniqueness and the auxiliary Mellin obstruction. General multiplier preservation and RH remain open.

A [certified complete-theta Mellin zero](theta-and-heat/theta-mellin-parabola-obstruction.md) refutes a proposed sufficient region for every individual conjugate pair. The [grouped multiplier theorem](analytic-methods/grouped-quadratic-multipliers.md) proves that suitable genuine real factors can compensate a failing pair; their required occurrence for the actual function remains unproved. The [quantum speed bound](theta-and-heat/theta-mellin-quantum-speed-bound.md) proves an auxiliary right-opening zero-free region using actual theta variance and projective Hilbert-space geometry.

The [derivative-kernel theorem](theta-and-heat/theta-derivative-kernel-logconcavity.md) proves strict log-concavity of $-d\Phi(\sqrt v)/dv$ for the complete theta kernel, with an Arb compact proof and an analytic infinite tail. Its shifted Mellin representation extends the quantum-speed region one unit to the left. These auxiliary constraints do not prove RH or Bernstein-function membership.

The [natural-exponent concavity theorem](theta-and-heat/theta-natural-exponent-concavity.md) proves $\phi_{\mathrm{nat}}'(s)>0$ and $\phi_{\mathrm{nat}}''(s)<0$ for every real $s>-3/2$. Its global reciprocal-score inequality uses complete-theta interval enclosures and an explicit analytic tail. The accompanying [two-crossing lemma](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/TwoCrossingMoment.lean) formalizes only the abstract non-strict integral comparison. Full Bernstein membership and RH remain open.

The [third-derivative theorem](theta-and-heat/theta-natural-exponent-third-derivative.md) further proves $\phi_{\mathrm{nat}}'''(s)>0$ on the same entire real interval. A complete-theta interval certificate, analytic tail, and three-crossing moment argument establish the result. The proof is computer-assisted and has no Lean formalization; it does not establish all derivative signs or a positive Lévy representation.

The [small-jump construction](theta-and-heat/theta-small-jump-levy-density.md) proves that the candidate Lévy density is positive and strictly decreasing near zero, using a uniform complex theta saddle and Bromwich inversion. The [global sign and positive-mixture criterion](theta-and-heat/theta-positive-jump-mixture.md) characterizes its extension to a nonincreasing Lévy density by one unproved global sign, equivalently an exact positive integral equation. These are written analytic results without Lean formalizations. The global sign remains open, and ordinary Bernstein or $B_J$ membership does not itself imply RH.

A [meromorphic complete Bernstein family](theta-and-heat/bernstein-theta-growth-nonreal-zeros.md) has the same leading exponent growth and double-logarithmic mixing-tail rate as theta, but its associated entire functions have nonreal zeros. The proof retains these asymptotics under a perturbation that preserves a nonreal Mittag–Leffler zero. It excludes that general real-zero mechanism without identifying the family with the actual theta law.

[Unitary prime-phase lift](prime-distribution/unitary-prime-phase-lift.md) gives a written joint-spectral extension of half-neighbor rigidity. Finite defect yields a self-adjoint scale generator, an exact spectral energy, and the form domain $\operatorname{Dom}|T|^{1/2}$. The proof allows arbitrary spectral multiplicity and distinguishes concentration on a Borel character set from a topological support assertion. For the raw exact-multiplication isometries and any support-preserving phase gauge, neighboring shift images become asymptotically orthogonal: every nonzero $\ell^2$ vector has infinite half-neighbor defect. The same obstruction persists after the canonical unitary dilation to $\ell^2(\mathbb Q_{>0})$: its multiplication maps are unitary, but all nonzero vectors still have defect squares tending to twice their norm squared. Neither counting representation identifies its form with the Weil form.

The [complete birth kernel](prime-history/centered-infinite-prime-birth-kernel.md) has two explicit endpoint tails and squared cutoff error asymptotic to $4/\log P$. ## Exterior poles and constrained theta elimination

The [complete return is compact and infinite rank](theta-and-heat/theta-killed-return-compact-infinite-rank.md), although the crossing operator itself is noncompact. The [local spectral-mass bound](theta-and-heat/theta-exterior-threshold-local-measure-weighted-source-bound.md) uses the exact complete covariance clock to show, at fixed core, that spectral mass in a band $I$ is at most $\sup_{s\in I}(s-\alpha)$ times the actual weighted-source ratio.  Its source is $f/\rho$, and its covariance identity depends on the proved equality of the original and rank-one-subtracted maximal potential domains.  It removes a bare small-denominator estimate for fixed weighted sources, but supplies no moving-core bound for that ratio and no signed-core positivity conclusion.

The [moving-radius weighted-covariance audit](theta-and-heat/theta-moving-radius-weighted-covariance-audit.md) supplies the part of that missing estimate justified by the published collar theorems. The endpoint ground estimate now gives $-\log a_R\le(\pi/2)e^{2R}+O(e^{21R/20})$. A localized terminal carrier with $\log K_X=o(X)$ has an exponentially small weighted ratio at $R=\beta\log X$, $\beta<1/2$, and also at the critical $R=\tfrac12\log X$ when its terminal support starts a fixed positive distance beyond the core. At the critical radius, support beyond the shrinking endpoint collar suffices with the stronger carrier condition $\log K_X=o(X^{21/40})$. This does not apply automatically to the actual Lambert field: the available source estimate is unweighted, and exact coefficientwise path synthesis has exponentially large carrier mass already on a prime hard-clock row. The [collective central-Gram audit](theta-and-heat/collective-lambert-theta-central-gram-audit.md) writes that remaining comparison before absolute values, with every angular frequency, quotient layer, and clock retained. The complete theta-side template keeps both orientations and every prime power in the residual covariance. On the exact hard-clock $N=1,2$ block, the Lambert coefficient vector approaches the antisymmetric line and its central energy is only $O(X^{-1})$ of its diagonal majorant; the common-mode line is coercive but does not contain the actual row. Positivity alone does not determine the corresponding theta difference energy. The [formal arithmetic floor pairing](successor-and-division/successor-feedback-tail-Abel-poles.md#formal-arithmetic-pairing-holomorphic-floor-remainder-and-age-endpoints) retains the shifted endpoint and full tail against the literal bounded $\psi$ source. Its exact remainder is holomorphic on $\Re z>0$, and both age boundary terms vanish with no assumed source limit. [FloorPairing](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/SuccessorFeedbackFloorPairing.lean), [FloorHolomorphic](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/SuccessorFeedbackFloorHolomorphic.lean) and [FloorBoundary](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/SuccessorFeedbackFloorBoundary.lean) prove these statements; the driver-to-zeta continuation remains separate.

The [literal successor transport of the additive Goldbach source](goldbach/goldbach-literal-successor-counting-transport.md) gives exact positive birth kernels, their full pair escape probability and an $O(X)$ shifted counting deficit from Chebyshev. Its nonnegative logarithmic pair correction tends unconditionally to $1$ by PNT; the remaining signed fluctuation is an exact cofactor convolution of the complete Chebyshev error with $\Lambda$. Compensation retains the linear and squared charge ports. The ordinary weighted comparison has a finite constant and remaining error $O(\log X/X)$; the PNT calibration gives deficit $2X+o(X)$. The source identification uses the classical Matsumoto–Suzuki explicit formula. No additive existence, global screw-sign or RH bound is inferred. [GoldbachSuccessorLogFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachSuccessorLogFinite.lean) formalizes the finite logarithmic correction, its nonnegativity and cofactor identities; the PNT limit and continuous transport remain written analysis.

The [signed successor cofactor source](goldbach/goldbach-signed-cofactor-volterra-source.md) has an exact finite Abel equation with its terminal prime-error atom. Its heat transform is the positive prime heat factor times an endpoint-corrected Volterra transform of the centered source; differentiation recovers that source. An all-$\varepsilon$ $t^{-3/2-\varepsilon}$ bound for this signed heat row is equivalent to RH, and is not established here. [GoldbachCofactorVolterraFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachCofactorVolterraFinite.lean) compiles the finite source and endpoint identities. The infinite heat limit and RH equivalence are written analysis.

The [short-interval actual-cofactor bound](goldbach/goldbach-cofactor-short-interval-vk-gain.md) proves $|Q_s|\ll_\varepsilon s\exp[-(2^{2/5}d-\varepsilon)\Phi(s)]$ with every prime power retained, where $d=0.212579202\ldots$ and $2^{2/5}d=0.280499938\ldots$. Its uniform interval estimate and adaptive dyadic shells improve the [earlier fixed-cutoff bound](goldbach/actual-prime-error-convolution-pointwise-vk-gain.md) of $0.2714144067\ldots$. The separately bounded centered term has exponent $d+J(d)=0.281822649\ldots$; $2^{2/5}d$ is the exponent of the separately bounded linear term, with no claim that it limits cancellation in $Q_s$. The proof uses Bellotti's 2024 zero-free region, Johnston's PNT transfer, classical Ingham density and a common-height Cully-Hugill--Johnston truncated explicit formula. The exact finite split is checked in [GoldbachCofactorLinearSplitFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachCofactorLinearSplitFinite.lean), and the generic endpoint-corrected Abel inequality in [GoldbachCofactorPointwiseAbelFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachCofactorPointwiseAbelFinite.lean). The analytic bound is a written proof, not Lean-formalized or an RH result.

The [signed cofactor crossing theorem](goldbach/goldbach-signed-cofactor-oscillation.md) uses an actual critical-line zero and Landau's positive-Laplace theorem to prove $Q_s=\Omega_\pm(s^\alpha)$ for every $0\le\alpha<1/2$, with arbitrarily long signed blocks when $\alpha>0$. The [uniform interval-sieve step bound](goldbach/goldbach-signed-cofactor-sieve-step.md) sharpens $|Q_{s+1}-Q_s|$ to $O(\log(s+1)[\log\log(3s+3)]^2)$, hence gives infinitely many sparse cutoffs with $|Q_s|$ at that scale; it does not give a uniform RH bound. [GoldbachCofactorSuccessorFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachCofactorSuccessorFinite.lean) compiles the exact source law, and [GoldbachCofactorSuccessorQuadratic.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachCofactorSuccessorQuadratic.lean) compiles the elementary $O(\log^2s)$ step bound. [GoldbachCofactorOddStepFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachCofactorOddStepFinite.lean) further compiles a one-sided $Q_{s+1}-Q_s\le2\log(s+1)$ when the new total is odd, retaining the power-of-two leg and endpoint. The sieve, analytic oscillation, and crossing conclusions remain written proofs.

The [strict odd-total drift theorem](goldbach/goldbach-odd-total-strict-negative-drift.md) proves $\limsup_{2\mid s,\ s\to\infty}(Q_{s+1}-Q_s)/\log s\le(\log2)/2-127/300<0$. Parity bounds the positive even prime-power leg by $(\log2/2)\log s$ and the other positive leg by $O(1)$; Guth--Maynard's unconditional short-interval prime theorem supplies a larger negative Chebyshev-prefix mass. The exact step with its prefix and endpoint is Lean-compiled in [GoldbachCofactorOddStepFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachCofactorOddStepFinite.lean); the analytic drift is written only. A parity-scale decrease does not bound $Q_s$ or exclude off-line zeros.

The [full-history refinement](goldbach/goldbach-odd-total-full-history-drift.md) improves that limsup to $\log(4/3)/12-13/30=-0.4093598272\ldots$. The exact $\psi(h-1)$ prefix pays every even prime-power birth from $h=8$ onward; only the $h=4$ contact remains positive. Guth--Maynard supplies the weighted prime mass down to exponent $17/30$, extended over the full history by interval subdivision and PNT. Trudgian's explicit $\psi$ bound signs the later power-of-two coefficients. The analytic refinement is written only; its finite successor identity is the same Lean-compiled identity. The odd-to-even Goldbach rebound still prevents the one-step sign from yielding an RH bound.

The [short-band lower-mass refinement](goldbach/goldbach-odd-total-short-band-lower-mass.md) improves the odd-total limsup again, to $\log(4/3)/12-13/30-3/800=-0.4131098272\ldots$. Baker--Harman--Pintz's quantitative prime-count lower bound fills the exponent band from $21/40$ to Guth--Maynard's $17/30$ threshold; the complete actual prime-power cofactor and finite endpoints are retained. Its analytic transfer is written only, using the same Lean-compiled finite step. The odd-to-even rebound remains compatible with off-line-zero-sized oscillation, so this does not prove RH.

The [mesoscopic parity compensation theorem](goldbach/goldbach-cofactor-mesoscopic-parity-conservation.md) proves, for every fixed $17/30<\theta<1$ and $H=\lfloor X^\theta\rfloor$, $Q_{X+H}-Q_X=o(H)$, with an explicit stretched-exponential window error. Over the same window, the sums of even-to-odd and odd-to-even steps are respectively $-\tfrac12H\log X+O(H)$ and $+\tfrac12H\log X+O(H)$. This is a signed short-window conservation law for the complete actual Mangoldt source: Guth--Maynard controls every shifted interval, and the exact finite successor coefficients telescope after retaining the moving boundary. The analytic window theorem is written only; [GoldbachCofactorWindowFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachCofactorWindowFinite.lean) compiles the exact window sum and prefix telescope from the finite one-step identity. An off-line-zero mode still changes by $o(H)$ on these windows, so this does not prove RH.

The [critical-scale signed cofactor theorem](goldbach/goldbach-signed-cofactor-critical-omega.md) compares the nonreal critical-line Mellin residue with the real residue of an eventual one-sided envelope. It proves unconditional $\Omega_\pm(\sqrt{s})$ excursions with an explicit positive constant separately on even and odd cutoffs. This sharpens the exponent of the earlier oscillation theorem; it is a lower bound and does not improve the uniform Vinogradov--Korobov upper estimate with an arbitrary fixed exponent loss.

The [parity refinement](goldbach/goldbach-signed-cofactor-parity-oscillation.md) records unbounded signed excursions separately along even and odd cutoffs. This conclusion already follows from the all-cutoff excursions plus the logarithmic one-step bound; the alternating source calculation gives a second, exact parity account. Both parity lattices have infinitely many polylogarithmically small crossings and arbitrarily long signed runs. The [alternating Abel main term](goldbach/goldbach-alternating-parity-main-term.md) proves the stronger global parity law $F(-e^{-t})\sim\log(1/t)/(2t)>0$ from PNT and the exact power-of-two support of even von Mangoldt weights; it does not sign individual $Q_s$. [GoldbachCofactorParityFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachCofactorParityFinite.lean) compiles the finite Abel bound, parity projections, and two-step envelope; [GoldbachCofactorParitySeries.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachCofactorParitySeries.lean) compiles the convergent cofactor product and infinite parity projections. [GoldbachCofactorOddStepFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachCofactorOddStepFinite.lean) compiles the finite alternating prime-series identity with every power of two retained. The note quantifies a crossing-gap bound that would suffice for an RH-scale upper estimate and benchmarks the uniform cofactor against Johnston's independently supported Vinogradov--Korobov prime-error bound with arbitrary fixed exponent loss. The PNT boundary asymptotic, infinite oscillation, and crossings remain written analysis; no RH upper bound follows.

The [formal discrete birth correction](successor-and-division/actual-integer-birth-positive-quadrature.md#formal-discrete-return-and-complete-geometric-correction) retains the exact return at and after birth, sums it to the central $\psi(j)$, and proves complete geometric double-sum convergence. Original feedback identification remains separate. The [causal root translation](successor-and-division/successor-feedback-tail-Abel-poles.md#formal-causal-root-translation) has exactly zero signed mass and $L^1$ norm $4(\sqrt{t+1}-1)$, with both boundary strip and tail retained.

The [centered additive circle consumer](goldbach/goldbach-additive-source-consumer.md) transfers the Goldston–Suriajaya positive majorant to the original frozen-source norm and complete clock action, retaining the seed and terminal square. Its subpower majorant hypothesis remains open. The [factorial-ground screw decomposition](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-ground-screw-increment-consumer.md) produces an explicit positive square plus the full ratio-prime, Hardy, gamma and contact residual; the residual has a nonzero $\varepsilon\log(1/\varepsilon)$ small-configuration term. The distinct [coherent-core screw readout](weil-and-spectral/actual-core-screw-increment-readout.md) retains the signed full-kernel observation. Its general screw-cone counterexample does not determine the actual zeta sign. All three consumers are written proofs.

The [complete time-ordered factorial birth correction](factorial-and-renewal/time-ordered-factorial-birth-trace-class.md) is trace class, including the seed and first density cell. It retains the actual later admission time for each pair and gives the exact prime-minus-density budget as its quadratic form on finite all-ones vectors. The trace-norm tail is uniform with the stated PNT decay; an explicit rank-one example shows why trace class alone does not bound the growing all-ones direction at RH scale. This is written mathematics.

RH Agent3's [finite global later-clock identity](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-global-later-clock-dyadic.md) groups all earlier literal births from any fixed $A\ge3$ before taking absolute values; the seed and first cell remain covered separately by the trace-class theorem above. [Heavy-age localization](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-time-ordered-heavy-age.md) sharpens each true dyadic block bound to $O_{A,c,\kappa}(Y\Delta_Y^2 e^{-\kappa\sqrt{\log Y\log\log Y}}/\log Y)$, but its absolute terminal sum still grows. The [equal-clock rank-one carrier](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-equal-clock-rank-one.md), [quantitative age estimate](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-quantitative-heavy-age.md), and [weighted-error audit](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-rank-one-weighted-error.md) identify a smoothed signed $E/(xF)$ score. The [prefix--block cross](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-prefix-block-rank-one.md), [kernel variation](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-moving-kernel-variation.md), and [later-clock drift](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-later-clock-rank-one-drift.md) feed the [complete finite scalar synthesis](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-complete-scalar-synthesis.md), which retains a moving ground kernel and non-summable residuals; they supply no sign or RH bound. The [zero-mode classification](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-zero-mode-rh-equivalence.md) proves that a subpower bound for its prime-minus-density coefficient is already RH-equivalent, while the [fixed-clock domain audit](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-clock-domain-zero-free-cost.md) shows that its sufficient weighted square-integrability assumption already forces a zero-free half-plane. These are written analytic results, not Lean formalizations or an infinite-terminal pairing theorem.

For that same fixed-clock spatial jump, RH Agent3's [polynomial-window escape](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-polynomial-observer-escape.md) proves power suppression of its newly excluded prime-power and density observations through every fixed polynomial window. The [infinite-future dyadic Abel reduction](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-infinite-future-dyadic-abel.md) cancels the artificial split endpoint exactly and isolates an averaged signed $D(x)=2\psi(x)-\psi(2x)$ correlation, with the coupled $F$ correction bounded by two heavy-age factors. That future correlation remains unsigned; neither note bounds the full time-ordered work or proves RH.

The [original-metric transfer audit](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-central-consumer-transfer-audit.md) records the finite frozen source, its terminal $E(N)^2/N$ physical-energy charge, and the distinct positive Möbius central-arc consumer. An actual-$F$ Hardy-bump counterexample shows that the signed Picone difference has no generic coercive comparison with positive energy; an arithmetic-specific transfer remains open.

The [off-critical Gaussian-mode calibration](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-offcritical-gaussian-mode-calibration.md) uses the exact factorial-ground score identity for a smooth oscillatory source with exponent $1-\alpha>1/2$. Its broad Gaussian log average is eventually negative with an explicit leading coefficient, so averaged negativity alone is not a universal off-line-mode exclusion. The model does not have the actual prime-power source; it supplies no actual-$\zeta$ sign, RH estimate, or Lean formalization.

The [dyadic Chebyshev Mellin audit](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-dyadic-chebyshev-mellin.md) proves that $D(x)=2\psi(x)-\psi(2x)$ has Mellin transform $(2-2^z)(-\zeta'/\zeta(z))/z$: it cancels the pole at $z=1$ but no nontrivial-zero pole. Its pointwise all-$\varepsilon$ square-root bound is RH-equivalent by exact upward dyadic inversion. The single moving $F$-weighted clock correlation is weaker; the full factorial correction remains, and a coupled non-arithmetic model with an off-critical power pole can still have power-decaying weighted correlation. This is a classification, not an unconditional RH-scale arithmetic bound.

The [unclocked dyadic-score inversion](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-f-ground-dyadic-score-inversion.md) proves that an all-$\varepsilon$ square-root bound on the actual averaged $D$ score at clock $s=0$ is RH-equivalent, including its unilateral boundary and integer-sampling conventions. At a moving clock $s=c\log Y$, the complete age law can attenuate hypothetical near-critical modes below that same response scale. This diagnoses the clock's spectral loss; it supplies no bound on the actual signed score or its coupled-$F$ correction.

The [centered Goldbach heat criterion](goldbach/centered-goldbach-heat-lyapunov.md) identifies the finite-horizon critical heat energy with an exact weighted sum of the centered coefficients $R_\Lambda(k)-2\psi(k-1)+(k-1)$. Its zero Lyapunov exponent and polynomial horizon-doubling condition are equivalent to RH. The [successor critical-scale calculation](goldbach/goldbach-successor-critical-scale-neutrality.md) proves that literal successor leakage is exactly of order $t^{-1}$ and therefore cannot be discarded from this centered energy. `GoldbachSuccessorTransfer.lean` checks the endpoint and compensation algebra. `PrimeStateAndRenewalArch.lean` checks the finite projector estimates and the exact maximizer geometry of each renewal cell. None of these results proves the open doubling estimate.

The [finite Abel boundary identity](goldbach/centered-goldbach-finite-abel-boundary.md) keeps both terminal terms when the centered Goldbach square is truncated. Complete centered successor leakage costs only $O((1-z)^{-1})$ at the natural cutoff. The transported signed covariance, the interior primitive convolution and the terminal primitive remain unsigned, so the identity does not improve the known $X^3\log X$ coefficient-energy allowance. [CenteredGoldbachFiniteAlgebra.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CenteredGoldbachFiniteAlgebra.lean) compiles the second-difference, prefix, two-terminal Abel and real energy summation-by-parts identities.

The [successor heat transfer](theta-and-heat/successor-heat-lyapunov-transfer.md) gives the exact Volterra transform of the full transported prime-power heat source. Its critical profile differs from the original by an unconditional $L^2$ function, so both Lyapunov growth and polynomial horizon doubling transfer in both directions. The doubling estimate remains open; this amplitude-level analytic result is not formalized in Lean.

The [original integer-birth feedback bridge](successor-and-division/actual-integer-birth-positive-quadrature.md#formal-identification-with-the-original-feedback) now identifies the existing relative driver with the complete $\Lambda$-weighted birth difference in Lean. The half-density integral and absolute countable Fubini prerequisites are proved, and the exact Abel identity retains $b_j\psi(j)$ at every clock. The original $E$ is unchanged; its near-one positivity remains open.

The [max-to-sum process comparison](dynamics-and-feedback/max-sum-process-transfer.md) proves the sharp $\pi/4$ upper bound and a reverse bound after complete prime-history damping, with the original frozen endpoint retained. Its RH equivalence cites the established fixed-clock original-source theorem. The [heat-generator identity](theta-and-heat/heat-generator-origin-flux.md) retains the negative-age origin flux on finite horizons and the signed birth pairing; three actual work intervals have an exact rational certificate. These analytic statements remain written, and the required subpower energy estimate remains open.

The [exponential weak ground row](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-exponential-weak-cross-row.md) admits the actual coherent-core sources despite their divergent individual theta norms. Its complete core-arrival and pole densities remain. The [odd sign-ground companion](theta-and-heat/theta-odd-sign-ground-exponential-weak-row.md) admits odd exponential tests, retains the signed core and cross-half arrivals, and proves exact leading cancellation with the odd pole difference. Its new PNT-scale bound handles the odd test's contact jump inside a low prime range and gives $\sqrt t\,|Q_{\rm W}(F_R,z_t^{\rm odd})|\le C_{R,\psi}e^{-c\sqrt{\log(1/t)}}$; its sign action is already matched by the covariance comparison, so this does not bound the remaining residual. The [fixed-core even PNT bound](theta-and-heat/theta-weak-ground-pnt-density-cancellation.md) cancels the pole kernel against the complete prime-density kernel and gives the analogous bound for $Q_{\rm W}(G_R,h_t)$ without differentiating the ground. The [coherent-kernel pole detector](theta-and-heat/theta-coherent-kernel-ground-pole-detection.md) proves that finite Cesàro square mean of this same full even mixed row, or of its clock derivative, along an unbounded set of fixed cores is RH-equivalent. Its real residue cancels exactly, so a detected critical zero forces unconditional sign changes in both rows; under RH, absolutely convergent zero series give their exact mean zero and square mean. The RH converse keeps all prime powers, the reflected shifts, the archimedean form and both pole products. The [continuous Green repair](theta-and-heat/theta-continuous-green-weak-row-repair.md) removes the actual slow resolvent mode by exact moment correction, but restores a growing correction for the scaling family. None of these results proves the needed unconditional mixed-row bound or controls the moving-core affine deficit.

The [odd coherent weak-row pole detector](theta-and-heat/theta-odd-weak-ground-coherent-pole-detector.md) performs the corresponding complete arithmetic transform for the sign-weighted ground. Its reflected prime correction and both pole moments are bounded, and an off-critical zero leaves a nonzero Mellin pole at every sufficiently large core through the odd ground moment. [OddWeakGroundPrimeReflectionFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/OddWeakGroundPrimeReflectionFinite.lean) compiles the finite actual Mangoldt reflection identity; the analytic transform remains written. Boundedness of this full odd row on any unbounded set of cores is RH-equivalent; no such unconditional bound or covariance-residual estimate is proved.

The [integer-history low-band identity](successor-and-division/integer-history-low-band-pairing.md) joins the actual weighted prime prefix and frozen endpoint to factorial forcing and complete Euler edge memory. Its Bernoulli synthesis retains the origin and terminal cancellation. The [coupled Bernoulli energy](prime-history/coupled-bernoulli-history-energy.md) exposes partial-divisor cross terms in the original max-kernel norm, with exact small-source rational certificates. Coprime and same-prime cross terms can be negative; the positive total and unconditional PNT bounds still do not control the missing RH-scale pairing. Both are written proofs.

The [shrinking even physical trial](theta-and-heat/theta-shrinking-even-negative-trial.md), with Trudgian's full PNT error, and [two-endpoint crossing bound](theta-and-heat/theta-internal-exterior-crossing-sharp-bound.md) prove [normalized and physical ground concentration](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-physical-mass-concentration.md). Using Baker–Harman–Pintz's quantitative short-interval prime lower bound, RH Agent2's [power-scale concentration](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-power-scale-prime-concentration.md) sharpens the actual ground collar to $8e^{-19R/20}$ at the endpoint exponent and proves $P_R/H_R^2\ge cR e^{-R/20}$ for all sufficiently large radii. The [endpoint prime-count energy bound](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-endpoint-relative-energy.md) improves the all-radius relative logarithmic-energy budget from $O(e^R)$ to $O(e^{R/20})$. Its [same-half suppression](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-same-half-prime-suppression.md) makes the complete same-half prime energy negligible relative to the odd pole mass. The [effective-width criterion](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-effective-width-sign-threshold.md) gives a conditional width that would pay the fixed $1/8$ sign-channel threshold; that width remains unproved. [Outward sampling](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-outward-prime-sampling.md) and [symmetric displacement](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-symmetric-prime-displacement.md) retain the exact weighted norm and signed hard-boundary correction.

The [ground-weighted Goldbach pair row](goldbach/theta-prime-profile-goldbach-pair-row.md) expands the squared actual prime-arrival profile into a complete additive Mangoldt pair sum with a nonnegative three-ground weight. It keeps the odd-total power-of-two sector and identifies the precise second-moment statistic inside the covariance comparison. At a density-one set of large endpoint radii, with exponentially summable exceptional measure, the collar contains no power of two and the entire compact pair row has even totals from odd prime powers. Proper powers contribute at most $CR e^{-19R/20}\|g_c\|_2^2$ to the compact first moment, but that bound does not control the ground-weighted square. Goldbach existence and current collar counts do not bound that weighted row or its full-profile tail.

RH Agent2's [moving arithmetic-gap arrival inequality](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-moving-log-gap-arrival-inequality.md) tests the full killed-ground equation on an interval between consecutive logarithmic integers. The stronger [microscopic anti-concentration theorem](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-microscopic-interval-antispike.md) combines one-sided hard-interval testing, disjoint near-prime translates and two-endpoint theta decay: every interval of length at most $e^{-2R}/4$ inside the $R^{-2}$ collar contains at most $C/R^2$ of the actual ground's physical $L^2$ mass, uniformly for all large radii. It includes the chosen integer-log gap components but does not control effective width. The [trial-and-crossing constant barrier](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-trial-crossing-constant-barrier.md) shows that the existing positive trial and two-endpoint operator-norm estimate cannot certify the strict effective-width threshold even under ideal prime density at the critical window. This limits that proof method, not the actual ground or RH.

The [common-score radius identity](theta-and-heat/theta-common-score-radius-transport-obstruction.md) separates physical energy contraction from the changing comparison form and optimizer. [Actual-grid counterprofiles](theta-and-heat/theta-lost-retained-prime-sampling-obstruction.md) show that the available concentration and logarithmic-energy hypotheses alone do not control normalized lost–retained prime sampling. The [mollified quadrature theorem](theta-and-heat/theta-ground-mollified-prime-quadrature-closure.md) gives a quantitative conditional repair, with uniform anti-spikiness and a PNT-compatible translation modulus still unproved for the actual ground. These are written results, not Lean proofs or a full-gap conclusion.

The [finite translation intertwiner obstruction](theta-and-heat/theta-finite-translation-intertwiner-obstruction.md) rules out compact restrictions of finitely many translated or reflected theta tests, even after finitely many bounded endpoint repairs, as coercive full-domain maps to the Suzuki interval form. [Summable infinite-tail maps](theta-and-heat/theta-infinite-tail-optimizer-span.md) also fail on the full domain by boundedness. The [minimum-norm source-normalized optimizer map](theta-and-heat/theta-minimal-source-normalized-optimizer-map.md) evaluates one named Suzuki matrix exactly and reduces optimizer payment to the sharp threshold $n[v_U]\le(1+\sigma k_0)/4$. Its rank-one full-span certificate fails strictly for the actual theta sources: the score-orthogonal direction has positive residual edge energy by the crossing-source boundary singularity and score-source tail growth. [ThetaMinimumMapConstant.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/ThetaMinimumMapConstant.lean) proves $0<k_0<1$ and positivity of both allowances. No current theta estimate proves the optimizer-only bound. The [optimizer edge upper-bound audit](theta-and-heat/theta-optimizer-edge-upper-bound-bottleneck.md) reduces the available ceiling to an exact finite Woodbury energy Gram. Its first unevaluated entry is the complete prime cross-profile energy, whose domain proof supplies convergence but no numerical constants; neither Suzuki threshold is currently certified. The [effective radius-two ground audit](theta-and-heat/theta-effective-ground-bound-radius-two.md) fixes the direction of the missing estimate: the published bound makes $1/2-\alpha_2$ small from above, while the barrier needs a positive lower bound. It gives a compact full-form Rayleigh and effective-barrier route to certify the required ground supremum without dropping prime powers. The [compact radius-two Rayleigh certificate](theta-and-heat/theta-radius-two-compact-rayleigh-certificate.md) gives an explicit even triangular trial for the full killed theta form and proves $\alpha_2<1/2$ with the effective gap $1/2-\alpha_2>143/(5120J(3))$. The proof retains the continuous kernel, killing, both prime orientations and every prime power; one actual prime already pays the archimedean energy. A numerical ground bound now requires an upper enclosure for the explicit scalar $J(3)$. The [rational enclosure audit](theta-and-heat/theta-radius-two-J3-rational-upper.md) and its exact-integer checker prove $J(3)<10^{544}$. The [rational gap propagation](theta-and-heat/theta-radius-two-rational-gap-propagation.md) therefore gives the unconditional explicit gap $1/2-\alpha_2>10^{-546}$ and fixes $S_0=5$ and $M=10^{500}$ for the ground barrier. [ThetaRadiusTwoRayleighCore.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/ThetaRadiusTwoRayleighCore.lean) compiles both polynomial convolution branches, the exact rational payment, and the abstract Rayleigh implication. [ThetaRadiusTwoJ3RationalCore.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/ThetaRadiusTwoJ3RationalCore.lean) formalizes every exact rational comparison in the $J(3)$ checker, the conditional gap propagation, and the barrier-amplitude inequality; the transcendental enclosure remains an explicit premise. The remaining non-effective step is now the fixed-barrier full-Chebyshev return and the resulting Schur constant $C_\varepsilon$. The [exact direct-edge certificate](theta-and-heat/theta-exact-direct-edge-scalar-certificate.md) preserves the common score projection, both crossing means, and all mixed prime/continuous Woodbury terms, reducing the plus allowance to $\Theta_{\rm edge}=c^*Gc<0.46511165192687$. [ThetaDirectEdgeFiniteAlgebra.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/ThetaDirectEdgeFiniteAlgebra.lean) compiles the scalar Schur residual, the degenerate zero-denominator case under explicit PSD hypotheses, and the complete three-column $c^{\mathsf T}Gc$ expansion; the analytic edge realization remains a premise. Its [coherent prime-continuous truncation](theta-and-heat/theta-coherent-prime-continuous-edge-truncation.md) expresses the first unevaluated entry $\mathcal C_P$ as an exact finite prime-power Gram head plus an explicit all-power tail conditional on three named analytic constants. No current estimate encloses that scalar below the allowance. The [validated killed-ground certificate](theta-and-heat/theta-validated-killed-ground-galerkin-certificate.md) specifies the residual, second-eigenvalue separation, weighted strong-norm, and interval data sufficient to enclose the ground and all eight optimizer Gram entries.
