The full umbrella build passes with only propext, Classical.choice and Quot.sound. This discharges the collision-integral and decay obligations in the linear birth-budget proof. Exact integer-birth identification and the pointwise signed-work telescope are compiled in [FactorialIntegerBirth.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FactorialIntegerBirth.lean). The complete divisor-pair parametrization, integrated linear total budget, full RH consumer, and arithmetic cumulative-work upper bound remain unformalized or unproved as stated in the source note.

- Literal additive factorial birth: `formalization/BuildingBlocks/FactorialIntegerBirth.lean` proves three exact full-history identities, including every divisor arrival, for all real clocks. The signed work bound remains open.

- [Complete divisor births and the signed LCM boundary](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-lcm-birth-boundary.md): thirty-eight added compiled theorems give exact complete-class cancellation, absolute full-clock integrability, and the actual integrated boundary decomposition. The linear integrated interior comparison and exact dyadic allowance are now compiled; the required signed boundary upper bound remains open.

- [Intermediate-clock signed work and LCM boundary upper](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-intermediate-clock-signed-work-upper.md): independently reviewed compensated Taylor/harmonic estimates give an unconditional O(N log²N) signed upper through t=N^(−1/2), with the larger clock open. Fifteen compiled theorems in `FactorialBirthInteriorBound.lean` establish the numerical full-clock interior budget and exact signed transfer allowances. The logarithmic response bounds are now compiled in `FactorialCompensatedEnvelope.lean`; their logarithmic integral specializations remain written and are superseded by the stronger compiled Abel clock bound below.

- [Reciprocal-prefix Abel clock bound](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-reciprocal-prefix-abel-clock-bound.md): twenty-six added compiled theorems prove the actual 3Nt response envelope, Q_N through t=1/sqrt(N)≤9N/4, and same-clock signed LCM boundary and common-dyadic upper bounds. Full larger-clock energy/work and RH remain open.

- [Actual physical scattering moment signs](weil-and-spectral/actual-physical-scattering-moment-signs.md): nineteen compiled theorems identify both original physical Mellin moments with literal finite-history coefficients, prove their opposite coefficient signs and uniform depth-dependent single-prime and list-block magnitudes, and retain exact varying-depth list-block Mellin and conjugate-polarized parity. Infinite gamma and full signed arithmetic upper bounds remain open.

- [Large-clock LCM source and terminal reserve](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-large-clock-terminal-reserve.md): an independently reviewed calculation gives the exact quotient correlation and terminal escaping reserve. Six compiled theorems now prove the finite LCM-class inversion, interior Mertens sum and nonnegative terminal boundary identity. A linear energy upper bound beyond t=N^(−1/2) would already imply M(N)=O(sqrt(N log N)); the analytic tail bound and required signed middle-clock correlation remain written and open.

- [Signed alternating theta response with paid arrival](theta-and-heat/theta-signed-alternating-response-paid-arrival.md) gives a finite-horizon upper bound by the exact continuous-minus-prime dual energy, retaining the common score, every prime power, both orientations, gamma terms and the affine normalization. [Original-metric arrival pairing](weil-and-spectral/suzuki-original-metric-arrival-pairing.md) proves that the Suzuki identity channel exactly compensates its arrival defect. The arithmetic smallness estimate and a Suzuki-to-theta form identity remain open; these are independently reviewed written calculations, not Lean or RH claims.

- [Full-composition Lambert–Mellin resummation](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-full-composition-lambert-mellin-resummation.md) resums every actual Möbius birth order and proves a uniform weighted L2 bound for the resulting signed all-horizon Laplace observable, including its original-clock tail. An explicit binomial family rules out polynomially stable inversion from the positive real ray; an angular response bound remains open. This is independently reviewed written mathematics, not a fixed-horizon energy or RH bound.

- [Finite sinc form and paid prime diagonal](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-finite-sinc-prime-diagonal.md): seven compiled theorems define the exact finite central-arc sinc covariance, prove the selected prime diagonal is nonnegative and bounded by the complete birth diagonal, and give the paid-remainder upper reduction. The analytic Parseval passage and signed off-diagonal estimate remain open.

- [Central-arc terminal obstruction](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-central-arc-terminal-obstruction.md) proves a written lower bound by the exact Abel-smoothed Mertens terminal value. The companion [Abel–Mertens RH criterion](prime-distribution/abel-mertens-rh-criterion.md) proves that uniform integer-scale square-root bounds with every epsilon are equivalent to RH, and hence that the still-open central-arc target A_X=O_epsilon(X^(2+epsilon)) would imply RH. No such upper bound is claimed.

- [Formal finite Abel–Mertens prerequisites](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/ActualFiniteAbelMertens.lean) retain the terminal Mertens term and mathlib's actual Möbius coefficients. Six compiled theorems prove exact finite Abel summation, the coefficient bound, the finite exponential derivative and its absolute majorant, and the positive consecutive grid gap. Infinite convergence, Mellin continuation and the RH implication remain written obligations.

- [Angular Poisson central-arc reduction](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-angular-poisson-central-arc-reduction.md) gives the exact full-clock circle representation, pays the outer arc by `(27π²e/32)X²`, and splits the remainder into its radial mean and nonnegative Poisson variance. A full derivative estimate is provably too large on actual prime births; the source-specific central variance remains open.

[ActualCenteredPoissonKernel.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/ActualCenteredPoissonKernel.lean) proves that each finite centered radial kernel entry $q^{\max(n,m)}-q^{n+m}$ is exactly a positive geometric tail for $0\le q\le1$. This formalizes the finite covariance algebra; it supplies no signed Möbius cancellation.

- [Low-clock central-arc bound](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-central-arc-low-clock-short-block-bound.md) proves an unconditional coherent short-horizon estimate by exact divisor-pair counting and Fejér localization. [Lambert dilation extension](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-central-arc-lambert-dilation-extension.md) extends it to the curved region `t≤sqrt(max(|theta|,X^−1))`; after the corrected pointwise shell weight, that region costs `O(X² log²X)=O_epsilon(X^(2+epsilon))`. The physical-clock complement remains open.

The [hard-clock LCM class audit](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-hard-clock-lcm-class-audit.md) proves the complete squarefree class is nonpositive after its diagonal is removed. [HardClockWeightedLcmClass.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/HardClockWeightedLcmClass.lean) compiles the actual prime-local identity, finite product bounds and the conditional squarefree induction; the general actual multiplicativity recurrence remains open in Lean. Soft radial truncation leaves positive semiprime rows of total size $\gg X^3/\log^2X$. The [odd doubling block](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-doubling-lcm-cancellation.md) identifies matching negative rows across adjacent LCM classes and reduces their terminal cancellation to a signed two-point Möbius correlation. Finite clocks retain quotient-history and proper-power reserves, so no $X^{2+\varepsilon}$ estimate follows.

The [analytic odd-doubling circle identity](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-odd-doubling-analytic-circle.md) proves the raw Möbius block has an unconditional linear Parseval norm. [OddDoublingFiniteMobius.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/OddDoublingFiniteMobius.lean) compiles the actual odd-doubling law, strict-lower annulus identity and finite geometric blocks. Successor division turns it into the exact annular Mertens energy $\sum_N M(N)^2e^{-2N/X}$, whose $O_\varepsilon(X^{2+\varepsilon})$ bound is equivalent to RH after the radial endpoint is restored. The [difference-successor commutator](factorial-and-renewal/factorial-difference-successor-commutator.md) shows why the raw gain cannot dominate the central consumer: radial and angular differences create exact accumulated boundary terms, including $F(e^{-2/X})$, and a coherent bounded-coefficient family disproves a boundary-free Hardy estimate with only one power of $X$ loss.

- [Centered Goldbach heat to Abel–Mertens](goldbach/goldbach-centered-heat-to-abel-mertens.md) retains every prime power and endpoint and proves that the all-epsilon square-root bound for the exact centered additive square is RH-equivalent, hence transfers to the Abel-smoothed Mertens criterion. The available unconditional successor leakage compares uncentered pair counts and does not prove this centered premise.

The [ordered-breakpoint and Fourier reduction](analytic-methods/progressive-future-cone-breakpoint-and-fourier-reduction.md) writes each fixed-set progressive prime admission increment as both an exact finite interval sum and a half-line Fourier energy with its boundary reservoir retained. A positive interval for the actual set $\{2,3,5\}$ and new prime $7$ rules out intervalwise negativity, while a one-prime frequency shows pointwise Fourier contraction also fails. The [native affine-Toeplitz realization](analytic-methods/affine-semigroup-toeplitz-native-tail-boundary-obstruction.md) reconstructs the centered Abel covariance inside the concrete successor-dilation algebra. Its negative Toeplitz flux is exactly the existing rank-one horizon-centering term, so it supplies no additional multi-prime contraction. Both notes reduce the remaining claim to source-specific affine cross-scale cancellation and make no RH assertion.

The [complete signed-core finite-block certificate](theta-and-heat/theta-complete-signed-core-finite-block-certificate.md) retains the indefinite mean column, negative weighted covariance, positive odd-affine correction, and finite prime observation in one matrix and gives an exact low/high spectral sufficient test. [ThetaSignedCoreFiniteBlock.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/ThetaSignedCoreFiniteBlock.lean) compiles the complete signed matrix and quadratic decompositions, equation (18)'s $c_*$, and the conditional nonnegative/coercive consumers without asserting its sign. The [independent finite-block audit](../reviews/literature/theta-complete-signed-core-finite-block-certificate-audit.md) proves that a usable Young parameter exists exactly when the signed low-block lower bound and complementary spectral margin are positive and their product dominates the squared perturbation norm. The [common-kernel obstruction](theta-and-heat/theta-complete-signed-core-cstar-obstruction.md) shows that on an infinite-dimensional finite-observation-null subspace the certificate reduces to the unresolved inequality (E_C[x]-\alpha\|x\|^2\ge a^{-1}\|\mathcal Sx\|_\gamma^2). Existing finite detection proves nonvanishing, not this quantitative covariance domination, so no nonnegative certificate or RH conclusion is currently obtained.

The [largest-prime packet Gram](prime-distribution/largest-prime-layer-gram-and-adjacent-correlation.md) partitions the exact affine bulk uniquely by each squarefree integer's largest prime and derives its full Gram, Stokes, adjacent-filtration, and Schur-complement forms. The required cumulative cross inequality remains unproved. The [dyadic layer analysis](prime-distribution/dyadic-largest-prime-layer-gram-and-bulk-reduction.md) proves a genuine negative adjacent-layer cross of scale (X^3/\log^2X) and an all-fixed-layer leading row-sum cancellation. Its [exact rational certificate](../formalization/verification/largest_prime_packet_profile_certificate.py) verifies (0.053<C_0<0.054) and (-0.061<C_1<-0.060). At finite (X), the uncancelled all-layer remainder is exactly the previously isolated affine bulk bound, so these results expose a cancellation mechanism without proving RH.

The [exact signed-core Gram contraction](theta-and-heat/theta-exact-signed-core-gram-contraction.md) rewrites the low block as (H_N=A_N-W_N^*W_N), keeping the two halves of the mean correction, odd-affine row, complete covariance, and finite observation together. Positivity is equivalent to a kernel condition and the contraction (|W_NA_N^{\dagger/2}|\le1). On the joint observation-null subspace it reduces to a true constrained-form margin paying the complete observation-invisible history gain and its affine-optimizer mismatch. [SignedCoreGramContraction.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/SignedCoreGramContraction.lean) compiles the U/W split, contraction/kernel implications, abstract pseudoinverse-on-range criterion, and optimized Young product test. The [optimizer-mismatch proof](analytic-methods/abstract-quadratic-optimizer-mismatch.md) and [OptimizerMismatch.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/OptimizerMismatch.lean) verify the exact completion of the square, the negative mixed mismatch term, covariance-ratio compression, and inverse Ritz lower bound. The [actual radius-two comparison](dynamics-and-feedback/actual-joint-null-gain-gap-comparison.md) removes the mixed loss on the even sector by parity. The [even-margin audit](geometry/even-radius-two-margin-and-gain-obstruction.md) and [EvenSectorMargin.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/EvenSectorMargin.lean) prove that the killed barrier does not supply the required full-space extension margin and state the exact restricted comparison still needed.

The [shared-reserve cancellation audit](theta-and-heat/theta-shared-reserve-cancellation.md) tests boundary-triple, observability-Gramian, and electrical-network readings of that restricted comparison. In every reading the same exterior covariance is subtracted from storage and from complementary gain, so it cancels exactly. [SharedReserveCancellation.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/SharedReserveCancellation.lean) compiles the cancellation, sign equivalence, reserve-shift invariance, covariance monotonicity directions, and scalar coefficient certificate. The surviving analytic theorem is the direct even core-to-port inequality $Q(Tx,Tx)\le E_C[x]-\alpha_2\|x\|^2$; current radius-two barrier constants neither prove nor refute it.

The [radius-two core-to-port trace certificate](theta-and-heat/theta-radius-two-core-to-port-trace-certificate.md) applies positive-kernel Cauchy before separating continuous and prime crossings. It obtains the sharper complete-column bound $Q(Bx,Bx)\le a^{-1}\int_CW_2|x|^2d\nu$ and reduces a direct proof to either the pointwise weight comparison $W_2/a+\alpha_2\le\kappa_C$ or its constrained Hardy version. Neither weight comparison is currently enclosed. [CorePortGeneralizedEigen.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CorePortGeneralizedEigen.lean) compiles the exact constrained finite compression, failure witness, generalized Rayleigh certificate, interval propagation, and trial/complement Young test needed for a rigorous spectral alternative.

[LargestPrimeSquarefreePackets.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/LargestPrimeSquarefreePackets.lean) formalizes the arithmetic packet interface with Mathlib's actual Möbius function: removing a prime divisor from a squarefree integer reverses its Möbius sign, the quotient is unique, and finite sums reindex exactly over supplied largest-prime fibers. The [formalization note](prime-distribution/largest-prime-squarefree-packets-formalization.md) records that primality, divisibility, and label bounds remain explicit hypotheses; it does not postulate a largest-prime-factor API or an analytic packet bound.

The [prime-admission flux analysis](prime-history/friable-prime-admission-energy-flux-and-log-average-obstruction.md) gives the exact finite Buchstab packet recursion and shows, using the actual `X=4` Möbius packets, that individual and positively weighted decreasing admission fluxes need not be negative. [PrimeAdmissionEnergyFlux.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/PrimeAdmissionEnergyFlux.lean) compiles the Hilbert energy increment, complete telescoping, weighted summation by parts, and resulting nonnegativity theorem. [KappaCoefficientPositivity.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/KappaCoefficientPositivity.lean) proves the exact affine-history coefficients are strictly positive, telescope through every finite cutoff, and have total mass one; the [formalization note](prime-distribution/kappa-coefficient-positivity-formalization.md) records the derivative and limit argument. The [Vaughan reduction](prime-distribution/friable-prime-cross-vaughan-reduction.md) retains the complete von Mangoldt convolution, prime-power carrier, moving endpoints, and affine histories; its surviving major-arc Type II term is a two-Möbius correlation at the same target scale.

The [finite-X affine bulk reduction](analytic-methods/finite-X-affine-bulk-corner-tail-reduction.md) completes both the clock and Poisson circle exactly, yielding the positive Gram kernel with weight `exp(-2 max(ad,be)/X)`. It pays the entire sublinear corner by `O(X log X)` in the normalized Gram, the tail beyond `X log X` by `O(1)`, and retains all cross-region terms through a Hilbert-space inequality. Consequently the far remainder reduces to the single growing-window bulk estimate `A_X^bulk=O_epsilon(X^(1+epsilon))`. The [radial and truncation audit](geometry/central-above-curve-radial-and-truncation-obstruction.md) shows that the alternative radial term already contains the Abel-smoothed Mertens value at square-root scale, while exact truncated Poisson orthogonality returns another signed affine correlation.

The [full-slope resummation](prime-distribution/full-kappa-slope-resummation-and-major-arc-positive-packet.md) writes `kappa_m` as an exact discrete difference and evaluates every finite rectangular slope sum as three factorial-logarithm boundary terms. [FullKappaSlopeFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FullKappaSlopeFinite.lean) compiles the difference identity, zero conventions, exact rectangular boundary, strict positivity, symmetry, and coordinatewise monotonicity; its [formalization note](analytic-methods/full-kappa-slope-finite-formalization.md) separates this finite algebra from the analytic packet asymptotic. The resummed kernel stays strictly positive, so closing the bulk estimate requires a negative prime--composite/composite cancellation. The [clock-mixer obstruction](factorial-and-renewal/nonlocal-clock-mixer-obstruction-on-KJ.md) shows that bounded logarithmic-translation-covariant Hardy, Volterra, Mellin, or scattering mixers either annihilate the actual late profile or retain separated translates incompatible with bounded preimages through the compact fixed-core crossing map. Its scope is limited to those covariant mixers.

The [Möbius--Stokes future-boundary identity](geometry/mobius-stokes-future-boundary-and-hard-reservoir.md) Abel-sums the exact future cone into its cumulative path `C_1,...,C_L`. Complete divisor renewal fixes only the terminal charge, while the hard-clock endpoint is `C_1=M(floor(Xy))-M(ceil(sqrt X))`. A positive hard-clock subregion gives an unconditional nonlinear capacity lower bound by the dyadic mean square of that reserve. The exact prime-dilation identity pairs an escaping upper face with a negative lower-boundary face; the unresolved input is their signed cross for primes near `N/D`.

[MobiusStokesFiniteBoundary.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/MobiusStokesFiniteBoundary.lean) and its [formalization note](geometry/mobius-stokes-finite-boundary-formalization.md) compile the finite Abel/Stokes identity with terminal charge, the complete actual Möbius divisor-renewal endpoint, and the unconditional prime-dilation two-face formula. The proof gives the literal $d\mapsto d/p$ bijection and retains the $p^2$-divisible zero terms, strict lower endpoints, weak upper endpoints, and $p\nmid m$ condition. It makes no capacity or asymptotic estimate.

[LargestPrimeFiniteWeightedStokes.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/LargestPrimeFiniteWeightedStokes.lean) compiles the exponential-tail identity and its exact finite weighted Stokes expansion. Packet coefficients may have arbitrary signs, all mixed terms remain, thresholds are explicit and nonnegative, and the finite slope weights are arbitrary. This supplies the checked finite algebra behind the largest-prime and future-boundary formulas; it does not estimate the infinite growing-window remainder.

The [prime-dilation capacity cross](prime-distribution/prime-dilation-capacity-cross.md) expands the two exact moving faces inside the nonlinear hard-clock capacity. It proves that a useful prime-band estimate requires positive average correlation between those signed Möbius faces. Pointwise favorable sign already fails at `N=146,D=13,p=11`, where the exact upper and lower faces are `1` and `-3` and the signed cross contributes `+6`; the [integer certificate](../formalization/verification/prime_dilation_capacity_cross_check.py) verifies every endpoint and Möbius sum using exact arithmetic.

The [Goldbach heat versus Buchstab audit](goldbach/goldbach-heat-versus-buchstab-near-collision.md) inserts the complete identity `Lambda=mu*log` with every prime power retained. Its exact local energy is the same two-Möbius covariance with two extra dilation variables and no undilated `(1,1)` row. Goldbach antidiagonals, positive heat kernels, density, and origin terms do not supply a local inverse; recovering the needed Möbius row would require a new nonlocal coefficient projection or a direct signed covariance estimate.

[PrimeAdmissionX4Obstruction.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/PrimeAdmissionX4Obstruction.lean) certifies the literal `X=4` bulk, actual Möbius values, singleton quotient packets, and both exact energy increments. Under the explicit retained positive-kernel lower bound, the first flux, second flux, and second cross-only term are strictly positive. The [formalization note](prime-history/prime-admission-x4-obstruction-formalization.md) records the precise finite scope; it makes no growing-X claim.

The [cross-horizon Möbius clock](factorial-and-renewal/cross-horizon-mobius-clock.md) derives the exact divisor-polynomial horizon increment, energy coboundary, and prime--horizon boundary-of-boundary identity with both moving endpoints retained. [CrossHorizonMobiusClockFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CrossHorizonMobiusClockFinite.lean) and its [formalization report](factorial-and-renewal/cross-horizon-mobius-clock-finite-formalization.md) compile the future-prefix reversal, actual Möbius divisor polynomial, floor-step endpoint, energy coboundary, finite Abel identity, and positive-increment upper bound. The reduction leaves a sufficient positive-variation estimate of size $O_\varepsilon(X^{1+\varepsilon})$. [Exact rational checks](../formalization/verification/cross_horizon_mobius_clock_check.py) exhibit positive energy admission and positive cross terms at consecutive horizons and verify the full Haar decomposition, so neither pointwise martingale contraction nor wavelet positivity closes the estimate. The signed positive-variation bound remains unproved.

The [Abel divisor-cube Stokes audit](geometry/abel-divisor-cube-stokes-obstruction.md) realizes squarefree divisors as oriented prime-deletion cells and writes the exact max-weight commutator and horizon-entry faces. Completing each LCM cube leaves the relative escape $M(N)^2-M(N)$, whose Abel sum differs from the original Abel--Mertens energy by only $O(X^2)$. An explicit largest-prime toggle matching leaves $\gg N^2/\log^2N$ rough prime-pair critical cells, so unsigned topology loses a full horizon factor and still requires composite opposite-parity repair. The LCM-fiber and terminal-reserve arithmetic is compiled in [FactorialTerminalReserve.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FactorialTerminalReserve.lean); the relative-complex and Morse statements remain written.

[CrossHorizonHaarFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CrossHorizonHaarFinite.lean) and its [formalization report](analytic-methods/cross-horizon-haar-coarse-obstruction-formalization.md) compile the exact dyadic Haar energy decomposition, invariance of every nonconstant coefficient under a common cutoff-gauge shift, and conservation of the root projection under recursive block merging. The actual Möbius clock specialization retains literal horizon endpoints. Thus multiscale detail estimates alone cannot control the unresolved coarse block sum.

The [fixed-profile Green admission theorem](prime-history/fixed-profile-green-admission-oscillation.md) proves $pe^{2p}\Delta_p=-\mathcal T(p)+O(1)$ for the complete limiting Boolean divisor packets and transfers Landau's two-sided oscillation to prime arguments using the Baker--Harman--Pintz gap bound. Thus these limiting prime admissions have both signs infinitely often. Its [full-face numerical audit](../formalization/verification/fixed_profile_green_admission_check.py) retains every old and delayed row. This rules out eventual admission monotonicity for the limiting kernel, but it does not transfer to finite-$X$ admissions without an exponentially precise uniform approximation and makes no RH claim.

[FixedProfileGreenAdmissionFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FixedProfileGreenAdmissionFinite.lean) compiles the complete actual Möbius packets, base/delayed faces, doubled-energy and perturbation identities, full Gram sum, absent-new-row fact, strict lower/upper split, endpoint tent identity, scaled $-\mathcal T(p)$ extraction, and fixed-packet sign stability. The [finite-horizon audit](dynamics-and-feedback/fixed-profile-green-finite-horizon-audit.md) shows exactly why pointwise packet convergence is insufficient: the sign margins are exponentially small while the packet and its divisor indices grow with $p$. No uniform natural-stage transfer is available.

[FixedProfileGreenTwoParameter.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FixedProfileGreenTwoParameter.lean) compiles the complete ordered-pair kernel discrepancy $B(X,P)$, admission discrepancy as the full kernel-error increment, strict lower/upper row errors, and exact horizon and stage telescopes. The [simultaneous-limit audit](prime-history/simultaneous-admission-discrepancy-audit.md) checks the proposed $-4\langle F_P,J_P\rangle/\log X$ correction and catches the required limiting-kernel factor $2K_G$. Its claimed uniform remainder remains a new unproved analytic theorem; fixed-divisor convergence does not supply the needed common PNT, sampling, derivative-jump, or rough-tail constants.

The [uniform second-log divisor envelope](prime-distribution/simultaneous-second-log-divisor-envelope.md) proves one component of that remainder with displayed constants: exact moving ceilings give row norm at most $3e^{-d}/d$, the full divisor packet costs $O(\log^{-2}X)$ independently of its number of faces, and the sampled Green norm is at most $10/\log^2X$. [FiniteDivisorEnvelopeSplit.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FiniteDivisorEnvelopeSplit.lean) compiles the exact low/tail split and domination by the complete integer envelope. The sampling and ceiling components are discharged below; the uniform PNT estimate remains open.

The [uniform sampled Green norm proof](dynamics-and-feedback/sampled-green-norm-uniform-proof.md) now pays the sampling part with a common $O(1/X)$ budget for $F_P^2$, $J_P^2$, and $F_PJ_P$. It retains the moving-boundary derivative and every breakpoint through explicit total-variation bounds. [FiniteSamplingErrorAlgebra.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FiniteSamplingErrorAlgebra.lean) compiles the finite rectangle-error and signed-packet consumers.

The [integer moving-ceiling audit](successor-and-division/integer-moving-ceiling-discrepancy.md) proves that the ceiling is exactly equivalent to the multiplicative activation condition and that changing the atomic endpoint convention costs $O(\log X/X)$ uniformly over the complete Möbius packet. [MovingCeilingFiniteAlgebra.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/MovingCeilingFiniteAlgebra.lean) compiles the activation equivalence, filter identity, single-boundary decomposition, and actual Möbius endpoint bound.

The [uniform rough-prime PNT propagation](prime-distribution/uniform-rough-prime-pnt-partial-summation.md) derives, from a parameterized classical zero-free-region estimate, a complete-face prime-row error at most $(800C_\pi/(c_\pi^2e^2))e^{-(c_\pi/2)\sqrt{\log X}}$, uniformly for $P\le X$. [FinitePNTErrorPropagation.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FinitePNTErrorPropagation.lean) compiles the finite main-count subtraction and boundary/interior error propagation.

The [adversarial non-endpoint assembly](analytic-methods/simultaneous-nonendpoint-remainder-adversarial-audit.md) checks every component in the literal common normalization and closes the remaining squarefree $X$-rough row by $2(\log X)e^{-X}/(1-e^{-1})$. It proves the uniform row error $O(\log^{-2}X+e^{-c\sqrt{\log X}}+\log X/X)$ and retains the correction square and both remainder cross terms. Combined with the second-Abel endpoint theorem below, this yields the full simultaneous expansion $\mathcal E_X(P)=E_P-4\langle F_P,J_P\rangle/\log X+o(1)$ uniformly for $P\le X/((\log X)^2\exp\{C(\log\log X)^2\})$, hence for every fixed $P\le X^{1-\varepsilon}$. [NonendpointRemainderAssemblyFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/NonendpointRemainderAssemblyFinite.lean) compiles the exact Hilbert-square and Cauchy error algebra. This is an unconditional asymptotic, not an RH estimate or a sign theorem for individual admissions.

The [final simultaneous Green assembly](dynamics-and-feedback/simultaneous-green-final-assembly.md) records the same expansion in one normalization and isolates the first unmatched extension: decay of the identical signed endpoint vector once $P^2$ reaches the Abel-effective scale. [SimultaneousGreenAssemblyFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/SimultaneousGreenAssemblyFinite.lean) additionally compiles actual Möbius vanishing off squarefree support, $|\mu|\le1$, six-row assembly, and finite rough-packet Minkowski. In particular, nonsquarefree prime powers have coefficient zero; the nonzero rough-composite support is squarefree and lies beyond $X^2$.

The [growing-stage consequence audit](dynamics-and-feedback/growing-stage-consequences-and-sign-transfer.md) shows exactly what this expansion buys. Whenever $P(X)\to\infty$ in the proved near-linear range, the normalized energy converges to the strictly positive fixed-profile plateau $E_\infty$ and cumulative admissions telescope to the corresponding endpoint difference. Individual admission signs require local row estimates. The [direct increment theorem](prime-history/direct-prime-admission-remainder-increment.md) cancels the common level remainder before norms are taken and transfers both oscillatory signs along suitable prime subsequences with $p\le(\log X)^{2/3-\eta}$. Its binding term is the explicit first density correction. [GrowingAdmissionSignTransferFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GrowingAdmissionSignTransferFinite.lean) and [AdmissionIncrementCancellationFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/AdmissionIncrementCancellationFinite.lean) compile the level and direct-increment algebra. Fixed admissions of both signs also give eventual finite-horizon nonmonotonicity. None of these results controls complete admission $P=X$.

The [first-density coefficient theorem](prime-distribution/first-density-leading-coefficient-and-cancellation.md) evaluates that binding term: $C_p-C_{p^-}=e^{-2p}(1+o(1))$, a positive scaled drift $p/\log X$. Exact counterterm subtraction or a two-horizon Richardson combination cancels it and transfers both prime-sign oscillations through $p\le(3/5-\eta)\log X$. [FirstDensityCorrectionCancellationFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FirstDensityCorrectionCancellationFinite.lean) compiles the counterterm, Richardson, scaling, and adjacent-pair algebra. The $3/5$ frontier comes from the literal full endpoint packet; unweighted adjacent admissions reinforce the drift, while signed weights introduce an uncontrolled two-point Mertens correlation.

The [signed endpoint-admission crossover](prime-history/signed-endpoint-admission-crossover.md) evaluates the endpoint cross that produced the $3/5$ norm wall. Before Cauchy, the old nonendpoint constant mode and the new prime-dilated endpoint collapse exactly to the additive Abel polynomial $Z_D(e^{-2p/X})$, where $D=(p^-)^\#$. Its near-complete first derivative is the nonzero product $\prod_{\ell<p}(1-\ell)$. This extends transferred fixed-profile signs through $p\le(2/3-\eta)\log X$ and identifies the next obstruction as a horizon-weighted correlation between $T(p)$ and primorial parity. [EndpointAdmissionIncrementFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/EndpointAdmissionIncrementFinite.lean) compiles the exact five-term endpoint increment, constant-mode collapse, Boolean orientation, and full cross-term bound. No claim is made beyond $2/3$ without that new correlation.

The [endpoint Abel counterterm audit](analytic-methods/endpoint-cross-counterterm-and-next-wall.md) subtracts that exact finite cross as a new observable and retains the other four endpoint terms. The modified admission transfers both fixed-profile signs through $p\le(1-\eta)\log X$. Its next wall is the positive terminal reserve in the endpoint quadratic, an exact weighted two-point friable Möbius expression. Two horizons reach $3/4$ only when their weights cancel the full first Taylor coefficient $a_{X,p}\log X/X^2$; geometric $X^{-2}$ weights remain at $2/3$, while exact Abel cancellation uses the same arithmetic data as direct subtraction. [EndpointCrossCountertermFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/EndpointCrossCountertermFinite.lean) compiles the four-term remainder, quadratic admission, common-source cancellation, and terminal reserve.

The [endpoint quadratic divisor-layer audit](prime-distribution/endpoint-quadratic-divisor-layer-obstruction.md) expands the next $p\asymp\log X$ wall into all ordered old/new and new/new Möbius pairs with kernel $q^{\max(x,y)}-q^{x+y}$. The top divisor interval is strictly positive, but the lower intervals contain the signed cross capable of canceling it. Second Abel removes only a one-variable constant mode, LCM classes do not determine the radial maximum, and complete-admission centering leaves the same weighted prefix-square increment. [EndpointQuadraticLayerFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/EndpointQuadraticLayerFinite.lean) compiles the finite tail reversal, divisor-layer expansion, and centered decomposition. A wider sign-transfer theorem requires the explicit weighted two-point estimate stated there; none is claimed.

The [mixed endpoint first-branch audit](zeta-and-zeros/mixed-endpoint-first-branch-abel-collapse.md) evaluates $2\langle\Delta Z,E\rangle+2\langle\Delta Z,W\rangle$ before Cauchy. For $D=(p^-)^\#\le X$, every admitted nonendpoint face stays on its first branch over the whole endpoint support, and the two terms collapse exactly to $2b_{X,p}(\log X/X)\{Z_D(q^p)-Z_D(q)\}$. Their first surviving moment is $(p-1)\prod_{\ell<p}(1-\ell)$, but the admitted-row scalar contributes $e^{-2p}/p$, making the mixed sum negligible at the crossover. For $D>X$, an explicit moving divisor wedge survives as a weighted two-point Möbius correlation. [MixedEndpointFirstBranchFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/MixedEndpointFirstBranchFinite.lean) compiles the collapse, first moment, and exact support inequality. The endpoint quadratic remains the binding wall.

The [post-branch double-Abel reduction](prime-history/post-branch-wedge-double-abel-obstruction.md) groups the old endpoint divisor $a$ with $pa$, exposing the exact cones $a>Xp e$ and $a>Xe$. It combines the mixed main row and endpoint quadratic into one ordered kernel, then applies finite Abel summation in both divisor variables. The result is a mixed second difference weighted by $M_D(u)M_D(v)$, where $M_D$ is the primorial-divisor Mertens prefix. The complete-admission background cancellation does not localize to this prime layer; the displayed bilinear estimate remains open. [PostBranchDoubleAbelFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/PostBranchDoubleAbelFinite.lean) compiles both terminal terms, their zero-total removal, orientation grouping, and the post-branch residual algebra.

The same post-branch audit now computes the mixed difference on the slanted boundaries. Each unoriented scalar residual has a positive affine boundary strip and a positive separable interior coefficient. The actual old-minus-new orientation is strictly negative on $Xe\le a<Xpe$ and has no fixed sign after both wedges become active. The strip widths grow as $X$ and $Xp$, while triangular masks remain, so this is not a uniformly low-rank reduction. [PostBranchBoundaryStripFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/PostBranchBoundaryStripFinite.lean) compiles the affine coefficients, exact boundary and one-cell values, interior collapse, positivity, and negative one-wedge test.

The smallest nondegenerate full-kernel matrix test uses $p=5$, $X=2$, and the divisors $(1,2,3,6)$ of the old primorial. Every entry of the symmetrized matrix is strictly negative, but its restriction to the zero-total divisor subspace is sign-indefinite: the exact witnesses $(-1,1,0,0)$ and $(-1,0,-1,2)$ give opposite strict signs. The [rational interval certificate](../formalization/verification/post_branch_kernel_p5_x2.py) proves the transcendental sign bounds for $e^{-1}$ and $\log 2$; [PostBranchKernelIndefiniteFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/PostBranchKernelIndefiniteFinite.lean) defines the literal kernel and proves the conditional-subspace consequence from those bounds. This finite counterexample rules out a uniform conditional positive or negative decomposition of this kernel family without an added correction, but has no asymptotic implication.

The [post-branch prefix Schur comparison](prime-history/post-branch-prefix-schur-obstruction.md) puts the mixed-difference form and centered consumer on weighted prefix spaces. On their common block $n<X$, the exact operator constant is $X\|A_{<X}\|_{\rm op}$ and the absolute Schur bound is $X$ times the largest absolute row sum. The full comparison has no finite constant: the centered consumer gives zero weight to $n\ge X$, while the post-branch form runs through $n<D$, and the exact $p=5$, $X=2$ kernel already has a positive mixed-difference diagonal at $n=2$. [PostBranchPrefixSchurFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/PostBranchPrefixSchurFinite.lean) compiles the finite Schur inequality and the cutoff-tail obstruction. This operator obstruction does not identify arbitrary vectors with actual arithmetic prefixes; the ordinary Mertens prefix and the primorial-divisor prefix agree only below $p$.

The [actual primorial-prefix evaluation](prime-history/post-branch-actual-primorial-evaluation.md) substitutes the complete signed divisor packet into the studied main-plus-endpoint kernel. A rational interval runner certifies positive quadratic values at all $p=5$, $2\le X<6$ stages and six displayed $p=7$ stages. For $p=5$, exact double Abel also certifies separately positive head, cross, and post-cutoff tail contributions; the actual packet excites the tail, with the cross larger than the tail. [PostBranchActualPrimorialFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/PostBranchActualPrimorialFinite.lean) proves the actual $D=6$ Möbius coefficient and prefix tables and its terminal-zero double-Abel identity. These finite signs do not control a growing stage or the additional mixed-row remainders.

The [literal first post-branch source audit](prime-history/post-branch-full-source-p5-x2.md) restores the open odd-prime and odd-composite terms at $p=5$, $X=2$, retaining the closed lower atom, prime powers, and all ordered divisor faces. An exact-rational interval certificate finds a positive correction of about $5.3406\times10^{-7}$ to the sampled main-plus-endpoint row. [PostBranchFullSourceFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/PostBranchFullSourceFinite.lean) formalizes the finite source partition and lower-atom algebra. This calculation covers that mixed-plus-endpoint row, not the total admission energy or a growing-prime estimate.

The [total first post-branch admission audit](prime-history/post-branch-total-admission-p5-x2.md) restores the old nonendpoint cross and both remaining admitted-source terms. A directed interval certificate, with an explicit tail bound for the infinite old rough source, encloses the complete normalized energy increase at $p=5$, $X=2$ between $0.0048519066314$ and $0.0048519066330$. [PostBranchTotalAdmissionFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/PostBranchTotalAdmissionFinite.lean) checks the full Hilbert-space expansion. This one finite increase does not give an eventual admission sign.

The [four-clock extension](prime-history/post-branch-total-admission-first-horizons.md) retains the clock-dependent rough coefficients and certifies positive complete increments at $p=5$ for every integer $X=2,3,4,5$. Its old/new nonendpoint cross is positive at $X=2$ and negative at $X=3,4,5$, so the total sign is not termwise. [PostBranchFirstHorizonsFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/PostBranchFirstHorizonsFinite.lean) checks the four actual primorial cutoffs and prime-square zeros. These finite signs do not extend to growing primes or clocks.

The [moving-prime fixed-clock audit](prime-history/post-branch-moving-prime-x2-sign.md) certifies a strictly negative complete increment at $X=2$, $p=23$, versus the positive $p=5$ increment. It also gives an exact old-row convolution and a uniform principal-mode reduction whose omitted packet is $O(p(2/e)^p)$; the surviving doubled-Möbius prefix and local Abel future have no proved sign. The rational interval checker covers every infinite source tail. This finite reversal rules out a universal positive admission sign, not an eventual sign law.

The [principal-mode split](prime-history/post-branch-principal-mode-x2.md) certifies that the first admitted mode already has the complete increment's sign at both $p=5$ and $p=23$. The later packet has the opposite sign at each stage but is too small to reverse either result. This isolates the finite reversal without establishing a growing-prime sign law.

The [stabilized odd-Möbius prefix](prime-distribution/odd-mobius-stabilized-prefix.md) identifies the old primorial coefficient below each admitted prime with a fixed convolution, while the prime endpoint remains $-1$ instead of the stabilized $-2$. Its local factors retain odd prime squares and have Dirichlet series $1/((1-2^{-s})\zeta(s)^2)$. [StableOddMobiusPrefixFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/StableOddMobiusPrefixFinite.lean) formalizes the exact primorial stabilization and endpoint distinction. No all-scale sign follows from these identities.

The [harmonic-endpoint audit](prime-distribution/harmonic-endpoint-cross-term-friable-audit.md) proves that valuation-Hodge projection cannot remove the binding endpoint. For every positive vertex metric, pairing the normalized harmonic line with the literal moving endpoint equals its coefficient mass divided by a positive capacity; that mass is exactly the same Abel-smoothed friable Mertens tail. The moving cut and harmonic projection fail to commute, and the $P=2$ endpoint already changes sign between its two lower faces. [HarmonicEndpointCrossFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/HarmonicEndpointCrossFinite.lean) compiles the finite Gram--Riesz, projection-commutator, and sign algebra.

The [second-Abel endpoint theorem](prime-distribution/friable-endpoint-second-abel-frontier.md), independently checked in the [adversarial audit](zeta-and-zeros/second-abel-endpoint-frontier-adversarial-audit.md), preserves the Möbius signs in the first friable layer. A second Abel summation exposes the exact zero moment $\int_1^\infty M(u)u^{-2}\,du=0$ and recovers the logarithm lost by rowwise absolute values. After retaining the floor term, summed PNT error, transition at $P^2$, and the squared initial-block energy, endpoint decay holds uniformly for $P\le X/((\log X)^2\exp\{C(\log\log X)^2\})$ with sufficiently large $C$, hence for every fixed $P\le X^{1-\varepsilon}$. [FriableEndpointCancellationFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FriableEndpointCancellationFinite.lean) compiles the constant-mode extraction, cross term, block split, and literal endpoint geometry. This does not reach $P\asymp X$, where the truncated zero moment remains nonzero.

The [near-complete endpoint crossover](analytic-methods/near-complete-endpoint-crossover.md) identifies that surviving boundary layer explicitly. For bounded or polylogarithmic $Y=X/P$, the normalized endpoint tends to a finite Volterra profile energy $\mathcal Q(Y)$ built from the truncated Mertens zero moment. After subtracting the coherent total mode, the exact residual energy is comparable to $X^{-1}\sum_{n<P}M(n)^2$; its all-epsilon decay is RH-equivalent when $X/P$ stays bounded. [FriableEndpointCrossoverFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FriableEndpointCrossoverFinite.lean) compiles the truncated-moment, coherent-residual, cross-term, and weighted-energy identities. The explicit background is unconditional; the residual bound remains open.

The [full boundary-layer assembly](prime-distribution/full-boundary-layer-energy.md) includes the nonendpoint profile and its indispensable cross with the endpoint. Its background is $Q_{\rm full}(Y)=2\|K_Y-F_\infty\|^2$. At full admission, the exact coefficient is $\mu$ and the zero-free Mertens bound forces $K_1=F_\infty$, so the entire leading plateau and density correction cancel with the exact factor four. Centering the original tail row by its first-clock common mode leaves a consumer $\mathfrak C_X\asymp X^{-1}\sum_{n<X}M(n)^2$, whose all-epsilon bound is equivalent to RH. [FullBoundaryEnergyFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FullBoundaryEnergyFinite.lean) compiles the full energy, cross, and cancellation algebra. Away from $Y=1$, the raw scalar energy alone does not recover the residual quadratic because its signed linear cross is independent data.

The [successor, history, and Goldbach audit](goldbach/centered-consumer-successor-history-goldbach.md) tests three natural attempts to control this centered consumer. It records the exact weighted successor recursion, the complete divisor-history identity $\mu*\log=\Lambda$ with every prime power retained, and the signed additive square behind a Möbius prefix. Successor observability, positive history domination, and Goldbach domination would each suffice, but their all-epsilon forms are equivalent to the same Mertens mean-square criterion. Generic path positivity loses two powers, multiplicative history still needs a stable inverse into additive prefixes, and the positive Goldbach square does not dominate the signed Möbius coupling. [CenteredConsumerStructuralFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CenteredConsumerStructuralFinite.lean) formalizes the finite positive-power recurrence, horizon splits, divisor history, additive coupling, and Goldbach square; it supplies no new analytic estimate.

The [Dirichlet future-observation audit](prime-distribution/dirichlet-future-mangoldt-observation-obstruction.md) sharpens the multiplicative-history obstruction. For every fixed positive odd `q`, an explicit Möbius dilation maps under the complete `2L` future observation to the sparse powers-of-two Mangoldt row, forcing inverse norm growth at least of order `sqrt((L/q)/log(2L/q))`. The same audit verifies that a Goldbach heat form pulls back to the divisor-incidence kernel `T* H T`, while the actual centered response retains its affine divisor-log cross and moving face. This rules out uniform bounded-inverse and scalar-multiplier arguments but supplies no estimate for the actual centered source. [DirichletFutureObservationFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/DirichletFutureObservationFinite.lean) certifies the finite identities and abstract inverse ratio.

The [centered Goldbach positive-transfer obstruction](goldbach/centered-goldbach-positive-transfer-obstruction.md) tests that sparse family and the exact successor identity against the Matsumoto--Suzuki centered heat square. The full square remains positive, but its first nontrivial even coefficient is already `c₄=(log 2−1)²−2(log 3−1)<0`; hence coefficientwise and centered-Hankel positivity fail with endpoints and parity retained. Sparse future observations obstruct bounded inversion but do not compare the two heat kernels, while successor transport preserves the RH-equivalent critical exponent. [CenteredGoldbachPositiveTransferObstruction.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CenteredGoldbachPositiveTransferObstruction.lean) certifies the finite sign and parity algebra. No unconditional target-scale Goldbach estimate results.

The [lowest screw and moment minor audit](goldbach/goldbach-screw-lowest-minors-audit.md) derives the dyadic `2×2` screw inequality and translates it through both the unconditional finite `H₁` explicit formula and the additive Goldbach prefix with its two error terms retained. [GoldbachScrewMinorFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachScrewMinorFinite.lean) compiles the screw entries, determinant, finite moment Cauchy--Schwarz, complete prime-power rows, and the smallest all-nonzero raw Goldbach Hankel counterexample at totals `4,5,6`. Screw positivity for the actual `H₁` function remains an explicit RH-equivalent hypothesis; classical Goldbach existence and known averages do not prove the minor beyond the absolute-convergence region.

The [dyadic screw remainder threshold](goldbach/goldbach-screw-dyadic-remainder-threshold.md) separates the Matsumoto--Suzuki weighted residual, the Goldston--Suriajaya circle-integral remainder, and the unconditional total Goldbach main-term error. It proves that a Fujii remainder `O(X^theta)` gives a vanishing two-scale error exactly for `theta<3/2`; at the critical bound `C X^(3/2)` the exact unsigned budget is `25C/2`. [GoldbachScrewRemainderThresholdFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachScrewRemainderThresholdFinite.lean) compiles this finite error propagation. The strongest unconditional zero-free-region input is `X^(2-o(1))` and supplies no one-sided screw inequality; all sub-`3/2` bounds quoted in the note are conditional.

The [equally spaced three-point screw minor](goldbach/goldbach-screw-three-point-minor.md) computes the full matrix on `t,2t,3t`, its cubic determinant, and the literal substitution by the weighted Goldbach prefixes at `N,N²,N³` with all three remainders retained. [GoldbachScrewThreeMinorFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachScrewThreeMinorFinite.lean) compiles that algebra, a nonzero critical-error sensitivity, and the raw Goldbach `3×3` Hankel witness supported on totals `4,5,6`. The remainder frontier remains exactly `theta<3/2`; the cubic minor adds no unconditional sign and actual screw positivity remains RH-equivalent.

The [first Bessel-product cumulant audit](goldbach/goldbach-mfunction-fourth-cumulant.md) derives the exact finite identity `κ₄=μ₄−3μ₂²=−(3/8)Σaᵧ⁴`, with `aᵧ=2mᵧ/(1/4+γ²)` and every zero multiplicity retained. [GoldbachMFunctionCumulantFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachMFunctionCumulantFinite.lean) compiles the independent-cosine moment recursion, nonpositive fourth cumulant, strict case, and the single-zero contribution `−6mᵧ⁴/(1/4+γ²)⁴`. Interpreting these quantities as time moments of `H₁(eᵗ)` requires both RH and rational linear independence of the distinct positive ordinates. The finite product inequality alone supplies no finite Goldbach constraint because the arithmetic translation loses the Haar phase average and retains an unbounded-time remainder.

The [centered Goldbach successor heat audit](goldbach/centered-goldbach-successor-heat-shift.md) and [CenteredGoldbachSuccessorHeatFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CenteredGoldbachSuccessorHeatFinite.lean) prove the exact source-index shift with its initial atom and upper endpoint, and the heat-shell difference as twice the signed covariance with `Λ(n+1)−Λ(n)` plus a derivative square. The literal additive coefficient changes are negative at total two and positive at total four, even though the shell kernel is nonnegative. The shift therefore yields no unconditional one-sided bound for the original scale increment.

The [complete-history Goldbach shell audit](goldbach/centered-goldbach-complete-history-shell.md) and [CenteredGoldbachHistoryShellFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CenteredGoldbachHistoryShellFinite.lean) insert the actual Möbius divisor-log histories into every bounded additive pair. Matching divisor labels give a nonnegative block with label `d|N`; distinct labels give a nonpositive correction pairwise, with exact first value `−2(log 2)²` at cutoff four and total six. This sign is arithmetic, but without a quantitative correction it leaves the centered heat scale estimate open.

The [adjacent-total Goldbach audit](goldbach/centered-goldbach-adjacent-total-faces.md) and [CenteredGoldbachAdjacentTotalsFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CenteredGoldbachAdjacentTotalsFinite.lean) derive the exact fixed-cutoff difference across $N,N+1$: a $\Lambda(n+1)-\Lambda(n)$ core plus explicit birth and departure faces. Certified adjacent even/odd pairs have opposite signs at cutoff three, even after weighting by the positive heat-shell kernel. For odd interior totals, the written power-of-two formula and classical PNT imply $c_Y(N)=-N+o(N)$ only as odd $N\to\infty$ with $Y\geq N-1$; no finite Lean PNT theorem or scale upper bound is asserted.

The [finite even-compensation audit](goldbach/centered-goldbach-even-compensation.md) and [CenteredGoldbachEvenCompensationFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CenteredGoldbachEvenCompensationFinite.lean) turn shell-square positivity into an exact weighted lower bound for even centered and even raw prime-prime packets. The odd interior deficit and signed moving-cutoff transition remain explicit. In the complete-source, then large-horizon limit, PNT makes the bound asymptotically sharp at the standard convolution main term; it gives no stronger scale estimate.

The [even-only cumulative criterion audit](goldbach/goldbach-even-only-cumulative-criterion.md) and [GoldbachOddPowerBoundFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachOddPowerBoundFinite.lean) prove the exact finite odd raw Goldbach power-of-two reindex and its uniform `2(log N)^2` bound. Consequently the odd cumulative sum is `O(x log²x)`, and the established full-average Goldbach/RH equivalence transfers to the even-only cumulative average with the same `x^(3/2+ε)` error. This is a parity reduction, not a new RH-scale estimate; the cumulative and analytic deductions remain outside Lean.

The [cumulative odd-channel refinement](goldbach/goldbach-odd-cumulative-asymptotic.md) sums the power-of-two rows before estimating them. Its exact identity and the quantitative prime number theorem give $S_{\mathrm{odd}}(N)=2N\log N+N\Phi(\{\log_2N\})+O(N e^{-c\sqrt{\log N}})$ for an explicit continuous periodic function $\Phi$. The binary period cancels under doubling, giving the eventual positive drift $S_{\mathrm{odd}}(2N)-2S_{\mathrm{odd}}(N)=4N\log2+o(N)$. After subtracting its explicit elementary baseline, the odd residual has a positive real-heat multiplier. Each separate one-sided bound at scale N^(1/2+ε), for every ε>0, is RH-equivalent. A known critical-line zero forces unconditional two-sided oscillation at scale √N log N. The exact successor law also gives a one-sided local slope bound and makes every odd-to-even step an elementary binary-power pair count; together with oscillation, the bound gives arbitrarily distant positive and negative blocks of length proportional to √N. These are an equivalent criterion and lower-scale constraints, not the missing one-sided estimate. [GoldbachOddCumulativeFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/GoldbachOddCumulativeFinite.lean) proves the finite reindexing, exact even-power correction, residual convolution, successor law, and local slope bound in Lean; the closed elementary baseline, PNT, heat, Landau, and sign-block deductions remain written proofs.

The [simultaneous endpoint formalization](prime-history/simultaneous-admission-endpoint-finite-formalization.md) proves the exact one-face identity $q^d-q^{2d}\le1/4$, the sign-free Minkowski packet estimate $\|\sum\sigma_dv_d\|^2\le |S|^2/4$, and the exact $2^{|Q|}$ divisor count for a product of distinct primes. [SimultaneousAdmissionEndpointFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/SimultaneousAdmissionEndpointFinite.lean) uses Mathlib's actual Möbius function. This is a finite unsigned ceiling; it supplies no Möbius cancellation or RH-scale asymptotic.

The [sign-free endpoint frontier audit](analytic-methods/sign-free-endpoint-frontier-audit.md) sharpens that ceiling with reciprocal-power face weights. For every fixed $0<\rho<1$, endpoint decay follows when $\log P\le\rho\log X\,W(2\log\log X)/(2\log\log X)$. The retained $\log\log P$ baseline supplies the factor two in the denominator; the range is sufficient, not maximal. [SignFreeEndpointFrontierFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/SignFreeEndpointFrontierFinite.lean) compiles the weighted actual-Möbius Minkowski bound, powerset Euler product, oriented cube pairing, and an aligned-vector obstruction showing that cube orientation alone gives no contraction. This is endpoint-only and does not close the full simultaneous discrepancy.

The [signed friable endpoint audit](prime-distribution/signed-friable-endpoint-literature-audit.md) checks de la Bretèche--Tenenbaum's current Corollary 1.3 against the literal divisor packet, Stieltjes endpoint, and energy normalization. Combined with the sign-free interior range, it proves endpoint decay for every fixed $P\le X^{1/2-\varepsilon}$, including the theorem's integer-layer transition regions. [FriableEndpointStieltjesFinite.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FriableEndpointStieltjesFinite.lean) compiles the finite left-endpoint summation identity and two-region square propagation. This remains endpoint-only; it does not supply the uniform non-endpoint remainder.

The [positive-kernel endpoint audit](theta-and-heat/theta-positive-kernel-cauchy-endpoint-obstruction.md) proves the complete-column Jensen--Schur reduction and tests its pointwise killed-potential certificate. The actual continuous endpoint corner gives $W_2(2-d)\gtrsim\log(1/d)^{3/2}$ while $\kappa_C(2-d)\lesssim\log(1/d)$, so $W_2/a+\alpha_2\le\kappa_C$ fails near both endpoints. [PositiveKernelCauchy.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/PositiveKernelCauchy.lean) compiles the real and complex finite-kernel reduction, combined kernel identities, and pointwise implication.

The [constrained boundary Hardy obstruction](theta-and-heat/theta-constrained-boundary-hardy-obstruction.md) shows that adding the internal core energy does not rescue this Jensen majorant. Exactly constrained even endpoint packets have pulled-weight cost $\gtrsim\log(1/\varepsilon)^{3/2}$ but complete killed-core supply $O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$, with every prime power and both orientations retained. [ConstrainedHardyObstruction.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/ConstrainedHardyObstruction.lean) compiles the finite biorthogonal constraint correction and cubic-versus-quadratic growth algebra. This rules out the positive-kernel Cauchy route, but does not refute the original coherent inequality $Q(Bx,Bx)\le E_C[x]-\alpha_2\|x\|^2$.

The [coherent endpoint-packet calculation](theta-and-heat/theta-coherent-endpoint-packet-port-asymptotic.md) returns to that original inequality. For the same exactly constrained even packets, the continuous port is $\Theta(\log(1/\varepsilon)^{-1/2})$, the complete prime-power port converges to an explicit positive finite diagonal, and the killed supply is $\Theta(\log(1/\varepsilon))$. Thus the coherent port-to-supply ratio tends to zero even though the Jensen majorant diverges. [CoherentPacketAlgebra.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CoherentPacketAlgebra.lean) compiles the complex norm expansion, mixed-term control, correction identity, and bounded-port comparison. Oscillatory, multiscale, and arbitrary constrained vectors remain open.

The [log-boundary coherent-port model](theta-and-heat/theta-log-boundary-model-form-small-coherent-port.md) handles those escape modes. In the endpoint coordinate $s=\log(1/d)$, the adjacent continuous crossing is the Carleman convolution with multiplier $c_*\pi/\cosh(\pi\xi)$, while the killed supply grows in both position and logarithmic frequency. The complete prime comb tends to a bounded diagonal and is confined by the same position potential. This proves arbitrary-tail relative smallness and global infinitesimal form boundedness/compactness of the coherent port, including all prime powers and both endpoints. [BoundaryFormSmallness.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/BoundaryFormSmallness.lean) compiles the tail, cutoff, and two-block algebra. Any coefficient-one failure is therefore confined to a bounded position-frequency block requiring a validated finite constrained Gram certificate.

The [finite constrained Gram certificate scheme](theta-and-heat/theta-finite-constrained-gram-certificate-scheme.md) makes that remaining bounded calculation explicit. A common biorthogonal subtraction imposes every real constraint before the mass, killed-energy, and coherent-port Grams are formed; the entries retain zero-extension killing collars, all prime powers, both orientations, and continuous-prime and prime-prime crossing covariances. In exact constrained spectral coordinates, the coarse sufficient test is $r_N+P_0/(\lambda_{N+1}-\alpha_2)\le1$, with a sharper Schur mixed-block test. [FiniteConstrainedPortCertificate.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/FiniteConstrainedPortCertificate.lean) compiles the two-block, quotient, and spectral-floor algebra. This is a reduction and certificate format, not a numerical certificate: validated killed-ground data, coherent port entries, and constrained eigenvalue separation through $\lambda_{N+1}$ remain to be produced.

The [validated finite-Gram preflight](analytic-methods/validated-finite-gram-prototype-report.md) implements that format with exact rational intervals, a five-hat even mesh, complete prime-power/orientation ledgers, and coherent summation before norm bounds. It reports **incomplete, with no numerical evidence**: the published finite-observation theorem chooses $M$, $J$, and its edge filters existentially, so the claimed scalar $\Re J,\Im J$ constraint rows cannot yet be instantiated without inventing data. [ValidatedFiniteGramEncoding.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/ValidatedFiniteGramEncoding.lean) compiles the rational coarse/Schur acceptance and exact common-row subtraction. The [runner](../formalization/verification/validated_finite_gram_prototype.py), [template](../certificates/finite-gram-certificate-template.json), and [preflight output](../certificates/finite-gram-preflight-result.json) refuse to treat missing entries as zero.

The [canonical observation correction](prime-distribution/canonical-finite-prime-observation-and-ground-input.md) resolves the notation collision: the source's later $Jx$ is finite-vector-valued, while the $J$ in $\mathcal O_{M,J}$ is a projection rank. If the complex observation dimension is $d$, the joint nullspace has $2+2d$ real rows; four rows are valid only for $d=1$, which was never proved. Canonical labelled dyadic edge cells replace the arbitrary filters while preserving coefficient one by Bessel's inequality and retaining all prime powers, orientations, and full-rate denominators. [CanonicalFiniteObservation.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/CanonicalFiniteObservation.lean) compiles the coefficient-one and constraint-count algebra. The [symbolic ledger](../certificates/canonical-observation-M64-L2-ledger.json) is explicit, but compactness still gives no effective stopping pair or positive margin, so numerical evaluation remains unstarted.

The [variable-row monotone observation certificate](dynamics-and-feedback/variable-row-monotone-observation-certificate.md) turns those canonical cells into nested orthogonal projections on one fixed core domain. Its valid order is $H_n\le H_\infty$, so one finite proof $H_n\ge0$ certifies the complete observation; the converse is false, and qualitative strong convergence supplies no finite stopping index. Nullspace implementations have $2+2d$ real rows and shrinking constraint spaces, whose separately chosen matrices must not be compared in Loewner order. [MonotoneObservationSchur.lean](../formalization/BuildingBlocks/MonotoneObservationSchur.lean) compiles the lifting direction, converse counterexample, margin-tail implication, two-block product test, and row count. The actual classification remains **incomplete**: no observation Gram, ground profile, or complement floor has been validated. The first available scalar input is the separately published radius-two interval $1/2-10^{-20}\le\alpha_2<1/2-10^{-546}$.

The [angularly collective transfer obstruction](theta-and-heat/angularly-collective-fixed-core-transfer-obstruction.md) proves that finitely many angular channels followed by the compact fixed-core crossing map have no bounded form-preserving right inverse on the actual late-clock shell sequence. For the infinite tensor map it projects hypothetical preimages onto the fixed limiting angular profile before invoking compactness, rather than incorrectly treating the tensor product itself as compact. This rules out the fixed-core transfer mechanism only; the nonlocal affine Möbius estimate remains open.
